# APP_URL: The URL where this applet is hosted.
# AI Studio automatically injects this at runtime with the Cloud Run service URL.

import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/vite';
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react';
import path from 'path';
import {defineConfig} from 'vite';
import {VitePWA} from 'vite-plugin-pwa';

export default defineConfig(() => {
  return {
    plugins: [
      react(),
      tailwindcss(),
      VitePWA({
        registerType: 'autoUpdate',
        includeAssets: [
          'icon.svg',
          'apple-touch-icon.png',
          'pwa-192x192.png',
          'pwa-512x512.png',
          'pwa-maskable-512x512.png',
        ],
        manifest: {
          id: '/',
          name: 'بيطار برو — العيادة والمتجر البيطري',
          short_name: 'بيطار برو',
          description:
            'تطبيق الطبيب البيطري ومربي المواشي وأصحاب الحيوانات الأليفة مع متجر مستلزمات متكامل.',
          theme_color: '#0D9488',
          background_color: '#F8FAFC',
          display: 'standalone',
          start_url: '/',
          scope: '/',
          dir: 'rtl',
          lang: 'ar',
          icons: [
            {
              src: '/pwa-192x192.png',
              sizes: '192x192',
              type: 'image/png',
              purpose: 'any',
            },
            {
              src: '/pwa-512x512.png',
              sizes: '512x512',
              type: 'image/png',
              purpose: 'any',
            },
            {
              src: '/pwa-maskable-512x512.png',
              sizes: '512x512',
              type: 'image/png',
              purpose: 'maskable',
            },
          ],
        },
        workbox: {
          globPatterns: ['**/*.{js,css,html,ico,png,svg,jpg,woff,woff2}'],
          runtimeCaching: [
            {
              urlPattern: /^https:\/\/fonts\.googleapis\.com\/.*/i,
              handler: 'CacheFirst',
              options: {
                cacheName: 'google-fonts-cache',
                expiration: {
                  maxEntries: 10,
                  maxAgeSeconds: 60 * 60 * 24 * 365,
                },
                cacheableResponse: {
                  statuses: [0, 200],
                },
              },
            },
            {
              urlPattern: /^https:\/\/fonts\.gstatic\.com\/.*/i,
              handler: 'CacheFirst',
              options: {
                cacheName: 'gstatic-fonts-cache',
                expiration: {
                  maxEntries: 10,
                  maxAgeSeconds: 60 * 60 * 24 * 365,
                },
                cacheableResponse: {
                  statuses: [0, 200],
                },
              },
            },
          ],
        },
        devOptions: {
          enabled: true,
          type: 'module',
        },
      }),
    ],
    resolve: {
      alias: {
        '@': path.resolve(__dirname, '.'),
      },
    },
    server: {
      // HMR is disabled in AI Studio via DISABLE_HMR env var.
      // Do not modify—file watching is disabled to prevent flickering during agent edits.
      hmr: process.env.DISABLE_HMR !== 'true',
      // Disable file watching when DISABLE_HMR is true to save CPU during agent edits.
      watch: process.env.DISABLE_HMR === 'true' ? null : {},
    },
  };
});

--- FILE: src/App.tsx ---
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import {
  BookOpen,
  Calculator,
  CalendarDays,
  Check,
  Cloud,
  LayoutDashboard,
  Menu,
  Moon,
  PawPrint,
  Plus,
  Settings,
  ShoppingBag,
  Sprout,
  Stethoscope,
  Sun,
  UserPlus,
  Users,
  X,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { AccountAuthModal } from './components/AccountAuthModal';
import { CalculatorsTab } from './components/CalculatorsTab';
import { DataSettingsModal } from './components/DataSettingsModal';
import { GoogleDriveModal } from './components/GoogleDriveModal';
import {
  InstallShareButton,
  OfflineIndicator,
} from './components/InstallShareModal';
import { OverviewTab } from './components/OverviewTab';
import { OwnerPortalView } from './components/OwnerPortalView';
import { PatientsTab } from './components/PatientsTab';
import { ReferenceTab } from './components/ReferenceTab';
import { ScheduleTab } from './components/ScheduleTab';
import { StoreTab } from './components/StoreTab';
import {
  DEFAULT_CLINIC_PROFILE,
  INITIAL_ACCOUNTS,
  INITIAL_APPOINTMENTS,
  INITIAL_PATIENTS,
  INITIAL_VACCINATIONS,
  INITIAL_VISITS,
} from './data/vetData';
import {
  ActiveTab,
  Appointment,
  CartItem,
  ClinicProfile,
  Patient,
  SoapVisit,
  StoreOrder,
  UserAccount,
  VaccinationRecord,
  VisitStatus,
} from './types/vet';

const STORAGE_KEY = 'vetcare_pro_workspace_v2';
const TODAY_DEFAULT = '2026-11-15';

interface PersistedState {
  clinic: ClinicProfile;
  patients: Patient[];
  visits: SoapVisit[];
  vaccinations: VaccinationRecord[];
  appointments: Appointment[];
  accounts: UserAccount[];
  activeAccountId: string;
  cart: CartItem[];
  orders: StoreOrder[];
  darkMode: boolean;
}

const TAB_META: Record<
  ActiveTab,
  { label: string; subtitle: string; Icon: React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }> }
> = {
  overview: {
    label: 'الرئيسية واليوم',
    subtitle: 'مواعيد اليوم، الحالات العاجلة، وتنبيهات التطعيمات',
    Icon: LayoutDashboard,
  },
  patients: {
    label: 'سجل المرضى والفحص',
    subtitle: 'ملفات الحيوانات والمزارع، الفحص الطبي، والوصفات العلاجية',
    Icon: Users,
  },
  calculators: {
    label: 'حاسبة الجرعات والسوائل',
    subtitle: 'حساب الجرعات الدوائية، فترات السحب، السوائل الوريدية، وموعد الولادة',
    Icon: Calculator,
  },
  schedule: {
    label: 'المواعيد والتحصينات',
    subtitle: 'جدول العيادة والزيارات الحقلية وتذكيرات المربين',
    Icon: CalendarDays,
  },
  reference: {
    label: 'دليل الطبيب والطوارئ',
    subtitle: 'القيم الطبيعية، فحص التحاليل المخبرية، وبروتوكولات التسمم',
    Icon: BookOpen,
  },
  store: {
    label: 'المتجر البيطري',
    subtitle: 'أعلاف ومكملات ومستلزمات الحيوانات الأليفة وحيوانات المزرعة',
    Icon: ShoppingBag,
  },
};

export default function App() {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<ActiveTab>('overview');
  const [mobileMenuOpen, setMobileMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [showSettings, setShowSettings] = useState(false);
  const [showGoogleDrive, setShowGoogleDrive] = useState(false);
  const [authModalMode, setAuthModalMode] = useState<'register' | 'switch' | null>(null);
  const [toastMsg, setToastMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [avatarError, setAvatarError] = useState(false);

  // Cross-tab navigation context
  const [selectedPatientForFile, setSelectedPatientForFile] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [openSoapForPatient, setOpenSoapForPatient] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [calcPatientId, setCalcPatientId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const loadPersisted = (): Partial<PersistedState> => {
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
      if (raw) return JSON.parse(raw) as Partial<PersistedState>;
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
    return {};
  };

  const initial = loadPersisted();

  const [clinic, setClinic] = useState<ClinicProfile>(
    initial.clinic || DEFAULT_CLINIC_PROFILE
  );
  const [patients, setPatients] = useState<Patient[]>(
    Array.isArray(initial.patients) ? initial.patients : INITIAL_PATIENTS
  );
  const [visits, setVisits] = useState<SoapVisit[]>(
    Array.isArray(initial.visits) ? initial.visits : INITIAL_VISITS
  );
  const [vaccinations, setVaccinations] = useState<VaccinationRecord[]>(
    Array.isArray(initial.vaccinations)
      ? initial.vaccinations
      : INITIAL_VACCINATIONS
  );
  const [appointments, setAppointments] = useState<Appointment[]>(
    Array.isArray(initial.appointments)
      ? initial.appointments
      : INITIAL_APPOINTMENTS
  );
  const [accounts, setAccounts] = useState<UserAccount[]>(
    Array.isArray(initial.accounts) && initial.accounts.length > 0
      ? initial.accounts
      : INITIAL_ACCOUNTS
  );
  const [activeAccountId, setActiveAccountId] = useState<string>(
    initial.activeAccountId || INITIAL_ACCOUNTS[0].id
  );
  const [cart, setCart] = useState<CartItem[]>(
    Array.isArray(initial.cart) ? initial.cart : []
  );
  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<StoreOrder[]>(
    Array.isArray(initial.orders) ? initial.orders : []
  );
  const [darkMode, setDarkMode] = useState<boolean>(
    typeof initial.darkMode === 'boolean' ? initial.darkMode : false
  );

  const activeAccount =
    accounts.find((a) => a.id === activeAccountId) || accounts[0];

  useEffect(() => {
    const root = document.documentElement;
    if (darkMode) {
      root.classList.add('dark');
    } else {
      root.classList.remove('dark');
    }
  }, [darkMode]);

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      const state: PersistedState = {
        clinic,
        patients,
        visits,
        vaccinations,
        appointments,
        accounts,
        activeAccountId,
        cart,
        orders,
        darkMode,
      };
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));
    } catch {
      // ignore storage quota errors
    }
  }, [
    clinic,
    patients,
    visits,
    vaccinations,
    appointments,
    accounts,
    activeAccountId,
    cart,
    orders,
    darkMode,
  ]);

  const showToast = (msg: string) => {
    setToastMsg(msg);
    window.setTimeout(() => {
      setToastMsg((prev) => (prev === msg ? null : prev));
    }, 2800);
  };

  const handleCopyText = async (text: string) => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
      showToast('تم النسخ إلى الحافظة بنجاح');
    } catch {
      showToast('تعذر النسخ التلقائي — يرجى المحاولة يدوياً');
    }
  };

  // Account Registration & Switching
  const handleRegisterAccount = (
    accountPayload: Omit<UserAccount, 'id' | 'createdAt'>,
    initialPatientPayload: Omit<Patient, 'id' | 'createdAt'>
  ) => {
    const newAccount: UserAccount = {
      ...accountPayload,
      id: `acc_${Date.now()}`,
      createdAt: TODAY_DEFAULT,
    };
    const newPatient: Patient = {
      ...initialPatientPayload,
      id: `pat_${Date.now()}`,
      createdAt: TODAY_DEFAULT,
    };
    setAccounts((prev) => [...prev, newAccount]);
    setPatients((prev) => [newPatient, ...prev]);
    setActiveAccountId(newAccount.id);
    showToast(
      newAccount.role === 'livestock_breeder'
        ? `تم إنشاء حساب مربي المواشي "${newAccount.fullName}"`
        : `تم إنشاء حساب صاحب الحيوان الأليف "${newAccount.fullName}"`
    );
  };

  const handleSwitchAccount = (accountId: string) => {
    setActiveAccountId(accountId);
    const target = accounts.find((a) => a.id === accountId);
    if (target) {
      showToast(`تم التبديل إلى حساب ${target.fullName}`);
    }
  };

  // Patient CRUD
  const handleAddPatient = (
    payload: Omit<Patient, 'id' | 'createdAt'>
  ): Patient => {
    const created: Patient = {
      ...payload,
      id: `pat_${Date.now()}`,
      createdAt: TODAY_DEFAULT,
    };
    setPatients((prev) => [created, ...prev]);
    showToast(`تم تسجيل ملف "${created.name}"`);
    return created;
  };

  const handleUpdatePatient = (updated: Patient) => {
    setPatients((prev) => prev.map((p) => (p.id === updated.id ? updated : p)));
    showToast(`تم تحديث بيانات "${updated.name}"`);
  };

  const handleDeletePatient = (patientId: string) => {
    setPatients((prev) => prev.filter((p) => p.id !== patientId));
    setVisits((prev) => prev.filter((v) => v.patientId !== patientId));
    setVaccinations((prev) => prev.filter((v) => v.patientId !== patientId));
    setAppointments((prev) => prev.filter((a) => a.patientId !== patientId));
    showToast('تم حذف ملف المريض وسجلاته');
  };

  // Visits & Vaccinations
  const handleAddVisit = (payload: Omit<SoapVisit, 'id'>) => {
    const created: SoapVisit = {
      ...payload,
      id: `vis_${Date.now()}`,
    };
    setVisits((prev) => [created, ...prev]);
    showToast('تم حفظ الفحص الطبي بنجاح');
  };

  const handleAddVaccination = (payload: Omit<VaccinationRecord, 'id'>) => {
    const created: VaccinationRecord = {
      ...payload,
      id: `vac_${Date.now()}`,
    };
    setVaccinations((prev) => [created, ...prev]);
    showToast('تم تسجيل التحصين وموعد الجرعة القادمة');
  };

  const handleMarkVaccineDone = (vacId: string) => {
    setVaccinations((prev) =>
      prev.map((v) => (v.id === vacId ? { ...v, completed: true } : v))
    );
    showToast('تم تأكيد إتمام التحصين');
  };

  // Appointments
  const handleAddAppointment = (payload: Omit<Appointment, 'id'>) => {
    const created: Appointment = {
      ...payload,
      id: `apt_${Date.now()}`,
    };
    setAppointments((prev) => [...prev, created]);
    showToast('تمت إضافة الموعد إلى الجدول');
  };

  const handleUpdateAppointmentStatus = (
    id: string,
    status: Appointment['status']
  ) => {
    setAppointments((prev) =>
      prev.map((a) =>
        a.id === id
          ? {
              ...a,
              status,
              visitStatus: status === 'completed' ? 'completed' : a.visitStatus,
            }
          : a
      )
    );
    showToast('تم تحديث حالة الموعد');
  };

  const handleUpdateVisitStatus = (id: string, visitStatus: VisitStatus) => {
    setAppointments((prev) =>
      prev.map((a) =>
        a.id === id
          ? {
              ...a,
              visitStatus,
              status: visitStatus === 'completed' ? 'completed' : 'scheduled',
            }
          : a
      )
    );
  };

  const handleDeleteAppointment = (id: string) => {
    setAppointments((prev) => prev.filter((a) => a.id !== id));
    showToast('تم حذف الموعد');
  };

  // Store Cart & Order Handlers
  const handleAddToCart = (productId: string, variant: string) => {
    setCart((prev) => {
      const existing = prev.find(
        (i) => i.productId === productId && i.variant === variant
      );
      if (existing) {
        return prev.map((i) =>
          i.productId === productId && i.variant === variant
            ? { ...i, quantity: i.quantity + 1 }
            : i
        );
      }
      return [...prev, { productId, variant, quantity: 1 }];
    });
    showToast('تمت إضافة المنتج إلى السلة');
  };

  const handleUpdateCartQty = (
    productId: string,
    variant: string,
    delta: number
  ) => {
    setCart((prev) =>
      prev
        .map((i) =>
          i.productId === productId && i.variant === variant
            ? { ...i, quantity: i.quantity + delta }
            : i
        )
        .filter((i) => i.quantity > 0)
    );
  };

  const handleRemoveFromCart = (productId: string, variant: string) => {
    setCart((prev) =>
      prev.filter((i) => !(i.productId === productId && i.variant === variant))
    );
  };

  const handlePlaceOrder = (
    orderPayload: Omit<StoreOrder, 'id' | 'orderNumber' | 'date' | 'status'>
  ): StoreOrder => {
    const orderNumber = String(1041 + orders.length);
    const created: StoreOrder = {
      ...orderPayload,
      id: `ord_${Date.now()}`,
      orderNumber,
      date: TODAY_DEFAULT,
      status: 'preparing',
    };
    setOrders((prev) => [created, ...prev]);
    setCart([]);
    showToast(`تم تأكيد الطلب #${orderNumber}`);
    return created;
  };

  // Backup Export / Import / Reset
  const handleExportBackup = () => {
    const data = JSON.stringify(
      { clinic, patients, visits, vaccinations, appointments, accounts },
      null,
      2
    );
    const blob = new Blob([data], { type: 'application/json' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = `vetcare-backup-${TODAY_DEFAULT}.json`;
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    showToast('تم تصدير النسخة الاحتياطية');
  };

  const handleImportBackup = (jsonText: string) => {
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(jsonText) as Partial<PersistedState>;
      if (parsed.clinic) setClinic(parsed.clinic);
      if (Array.isArray(parsed.patients)) setPatients(parsed.patients);
      if (Array.isArray(parsed.visits)) setVisits(parsed.visits);
      if (Array.isArray(parsed.vaccinations))
        setVaccinations(parsed.vaccinations);
      if (Array.isArray(parsed.appointments))
        setAppointments(parsed.appointments);
      if (Array.isArray(parsed.accounts)) setAccounts(parsed.accounts);
      showToast('تم استعادة البيانات بنجاح');
      setShowSettings(false);
    } catch {
      showToast('ملف النسخة الاحتياطية غير صالح');
    }
  };

  const handleResetDemoData = () => {
    setClinic(DEFAULT_CLINIC_PROFILE);
    setPatients(INITIAL_PATIENTS);
    setVisits(INITIAL_VISITS);
    setVaccinations(INITIAL_VACCINATIONS);
    setAppointments(INITIAL_APPOINTMENTS);
    setAccounts(INITIAL_ACCOUNTS);
    setActiveAccountId(INITIAL_ACCOUNTS[0].id);
    showToast('تمت استعادة البيانات الافتراضية');
  };

  // Navigation Helpers
  const handleStartExamForPatient = (patientId: string) => {
    setOpenSoapForPatient(patientId);
    setActiveTab('patients');
  };

  const handleOpenDosageForPatient = (patientId: string) => {
    setCalcPatientId(patientId);
    setActiveTab('calculators');
  };

  const currentTabMeta = TAB_META[activeTab];

  // Dynamic Simple Role Theme Styling
  const roleTheme =
    activeAccount.role === 'livestock_breeder'
      ? {
          pageBg: 'bg-[#F4FAF6] dark:bg-slate-950',
          brandBox: 'bg-emerald-600 text-white',
          roleLabel: 'مربي مواشي',
          roleBadgeText: 'text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400',
          primaryBtn: 'bg-emerald-600 text-white hover:bg-emerald-700',
          RoleIcon: Sprout,
        }
      : activeAccount.role === 'pet_owner'
      ? {
          pageBg: 'bg-[#FFFDF5] dark:bg-slate-950',
          brandBox: 'bg-yellow-400 text-slate-950',
          roleLabel: 'صاحب حيوان أليف',
          roleBadgeText: 'text-yellow-800 dark:text-yellow-300',
          primaryBtn: 'bg-yellow-400 text-slate-950 font-bold hover:bg-yellow-500',
          RoleIcon: PawPrint,
        }
      : {
          pageBg: 'bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950',
          brandBox: 'bg-teal-600 text-white',
          roleLabel: 'الطبيب البيطري',
          roleBadgeText: 'text-teal-700 dark:text-teal-400',
          primaryBtn: 'bg-teal-600 text-white hover:bg-teal-700',
          RoleIcon: Stethoscope,
        };

  const RoleIcon = roleTheme.RoleIcon;

  return (
    <div
      className={`min-h-screen text-slate-900 transition-colors dark:text-slate-100 ${roleTheme.pageBg}`}
    >
      <div className="flex min-h-screen">
        {/* Mobile Sidebar Backdrop */}
        {mobileMenuOpen && (
          <div
            onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}
            className="no-print fixed inset-0 z-30 bg-slate-950/50 lg:hidden"
          />
        )}

        {/* Clean Simple Sidebar */}
        <aside
          className={`no-print fixed inset-y-0 right-0 z-40 flex w-64 shrink-0 flex-col border-l border-slate-200 bg-white transition-transform duration-200 lg:static lg:translate-x-0 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900 ${
            mobileMenuOpen ? 'translate-x-0' : 'translate-x-full lg:translate-x-0'
          }`}
        >
          {/* Brand Wordmark */}
          <div className="flex h-16 items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-200 px-5 dark:border-slate-800">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
              <div
                className={`flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-xl ${roleTheme.brandBox}`}
              >
                <RoleIcon className="h-5 w-5" />
              </div>
              <span className="font-display text-lg font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900 whitespace-nowrap dark:text-slate-50">
                بيطار برو
              </span>
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}
              className="rounded-lg p-1 text-slate-500 lg:hidden"
            >
              <X className="h-5 w-5" />
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Navigation Links (Vet Workspace vs Owner/Breeder Portal) */}
          {activeAccount.role === 'vet' ? (
            <nav className="flex-1 space-y-1.5 p-3" aria-label="التنقل الرئيسي">
              {(Object.keys(TAB_META) as ActiveTab[]).map((key) => {
                const item = TAB_META[key];
                const Icon = item.Icon;
                const isActive = activeTab === key;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={key}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setActiveTab(key);
                      setMobileMenuOpen(false);
                    }}
                    className={`flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-3 text-right text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${
                      isActive
                        ? 'bg-teal-50 font-semibold text-teal-800 dark:bg-teal-950/50 dark:text-teal-300'
                        : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-400 dark:hover:bg-slate-800/60 dark:hover:text-slate-100'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <Icon
                      className={`h-4 w-4 shrink-0 ${
                        isActive
                          ? 'text-teal-700 dark:text-teal-400'
                          : 'text-slate-400'
                      }`}
                    />
                    <span className="truncate">{item.label}</span>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </nav>
          ) : (
            <div className="flex-1 space-y-3 p-4">
              <div
                className={`rounded-2xl border p-4 ${
                  activeAccount.role === 'livestock_breeder'
                    ? 'border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50/70 dark:border-emerald-900/50 dark:bg-emerald-950/30'
                    : 'border-yellow-300 bg-yellow-50/80 dark:border-yellow-700/50 dark:bg-yellow-950/30'
                }`}
              >
                <div
                  className={`text-xs font-bold ${roleTheme.roleBadgeText}`}
                >
                  {roleTheme.roleLabel}
                </div>
                <div className="mt-1 text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                  {activeAccount.fullName}
                </div>
                <p className="mt-1.5 text-xs leading-relaxed text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
                  {activeAccount.role === 'livestock_breeder'
                    ? 'حساب مخصص لمتابعة قطيعك وتحصيناته وطلب الزيارات الميدانية بسهولة.'
                    : 'حساب مخصص لمتابعة صحة حيوانك الأليف ومواعيد تطعيماته وحجز العيادة.'}
                </p>
              </div>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setAuthModalMode('switch');
                  setMobileMenuOpen(false);
                }}
                className="flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-50 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200"
              >
                <Stethoscope className="h-4 w-4 text-teal-600" />
                العودة لحساب الطبيب البيطري
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Account & Clinic Footer */}
          <div className="border-t border-slate-200 p-4 dark:border-slate-800">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              {activeAccount.role === 'vet' && !avatarError ? (
                <img
                  src="/src/assets/images/vet_doctor_avatar_1794753637609.jpg"
                  alt={activeAccount.fullName}
                  referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
                  onError={() => setAvatarError(true)}
                  className="h-10 w-10 shrink-0 rounded-full border border-slate-200 object-cover dark:border-slate-700"
                />
              ) : (
                <div
                  className={`flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full ${roleTheme.brandBox}`}
                >
                  <RoleIcon className="h-5 w-5" />
                </div>
              )}
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <div className="truncate text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                  {activeAccount.fullName}
                </div>
                <div
                  className={`truncate text-xs font-medium ${roleTheme.roleBadgeText}`}
                >
                  {roleTheme.roleLabel}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setAuthModalMode('switch')}
                className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-2.5 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-50 dark:border-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 dark:hover:bg-slate-800"
              >
                تبديل الحساب
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowSettings(true)}
                className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-2.5 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-50 dark:border-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 dark:hover:bg-slate-800"
              >
                <Settings className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                الإعدادات
              </button>
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setShowGoogleDrive(true)}
              className="mt-2 flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-teal-200 bg-teal-50/60 px-2.5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-teal-800 transition-colors hover:bg-teal-100/70 dark:border-teal-900/60 dark:bg-teal-950/40 dark:text-teal-300"
            >
              <Cloud className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              قاعدة بيانات Google Drive
            </button>
          </div>
        </aside>

        {/* Main Workspace Area */}
        <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col">
          {/* Clean Simple Top Bar */}
          <header className="no-print sticky top-0 z-20 flex h-16 items-center justify-between gap-4 border-b border-slate-200 bg-white/95 px-4 backdrop-blur-xs sm:px-6 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900/95">
            <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(true)}
                aria-label="فتح القائمة"
                className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-2 text-slate-600 lg:hidden dark:border-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300"
              >
                <Menu className="h-4 w-4" />
              </button>
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                  <span className="truncate font-medium">{clinic.clinicName}</span>
                  <span aria-hidden="true">/</span>
                  <span
                    className={`font-semibold whitespace-nowrap ${roleTheme.roleBadgeText}`}
                  >
                    {activeAccount.role === 'vet'
                      ? currentTabMeta.label
                      : roleTheme.roleLabel}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2.5">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setDarkMode((d) => !d)}
                aria-label="تبديل المظهر الداكن والفاتح"
                className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-2 text-slate-600 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 dark:hover:bg-slate-800"
              >
                {darkMode ? (
                  <Sun className="h-4 w-4" />
                ) : (
                  <Moon className="h-4 w-4" />
                )}
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowGoogleDrive(true)}
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-50 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-700"
              >
                <Cloud className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-teal-600 dark:text-teal-400" />
                <span className="hidden sm:inline">Google Drive</span>
              </button>

              <InstallShareButton
                clinicName={clinic.clinicName}
                onCopyText={handleCopyText}
              />

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setAuthModalMode('register')}
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-50 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-700"
              >
                <UserPlus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                تسجيل حساب جديد
              </button>

              {activeAccount.role === 'vet' && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setActiveTab('patients')}
                  className={`hidden whitespace-nowrap rounded-xl px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors sm:inline-flex sm:items-center sm:gap-1.5 ${roleTheme.primaryBtn}`}
                >
                  <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                  ملف مريض جديد
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          </header>

          {/* Content Viewport */}
          <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl flex-1 px-4 py-6 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
            {activeAccount.role === 'vet' ? (
              <>
                <div className="mb-6">
                  <h1 className="font-display text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 text-balance dark:text-slate-50">
                    {currentTabMeta.label}
                  </h1>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
                    {currentTabMeta.subtitle}
                  </p>
                </div>

                {activeTab === 'overview' && (
                  <OverviewTab
                    todayStr={TODAY_DEFAULT}
                    patients={patients}
                    appointments={appointments}
                    visits={visits}
                    vaccinations={vaccinations}
                    onUpdateVisitStatus={handleUpdateVisitStatus}
                    onStartExam={handleStartExamForPatient}
                    onOpenDosageForPatient={handleOpenDosageForPatient}
                    onCopyReminder={handleCopyText}
                    onNavigatePatients={() => setActiveTab('patients')}
                    onNavigateSchedule={() => setActiveTab('schedule')}
                    onMarkVaccineDone={handleMarkVaccineDone}
                  />
                )}

                {activeTab === 'patients' && (
                  <PatientsTab
                    todayStr={TODAY_DEFAULT}
                    clinic={clinic}
                    patients={patients}
                    visits={visits}
                    vaccinations={vaccinations}
                    initialPatientId={selectedPatientForFile}
                    openNewSoapForPatientId={openSoapForPatient}
                    onClearInitialAction={() => {
                      setSelectedPatientForFile(null);
                      setOpenSoapForPatient(null);
                    }}
                    onAddPatient={handleAddPatient}
                    onUpdatePatient={handleUpdatePatient}
                    onDeletePatient={handleDeletePatient}
                    onAddVisit={handleAddVisit}
                    onAddVaccination={handleAddVaccination}
                    onOpenDosageForPatient={handleOpenDosageForPatient}
                  />
                )}

                {activeTab === 'calculators' && (
                  <CalculatorsTab
                    patients={patients}
                    preselectedPatientId={calcPatientId}
                    onCopyText={handleCopyText}
                  />
                )}

                {activeTab === 'schedule' && (
                  <ScheduleTab
                    todayStr={TODAY_DEFAULT}
                    patients={patients}
                    appointments={appointments}
                    vaccinations={vaccinations}
                    onAddAppointment={handleAddAppointment}
                    onUpdateAppointmentStatus={handleUpdateAppointmentStatus}
                    onDeleteAppointment={handleDeleteAppointment}
                    onMarkVaccineDone={handleMarkVaccineDone}
                    onCopyReminder={handleCopyText}
                  />
                )}

                {activeTab === 'reference' && <ReferenceTab />}

                {activeTab === 'store' && (
                  <StoreTab
                    todayStr={TODAY_DEFAULT}
                    activeAccount={activeAccount}
                    cart={cart}
                    orders={orders}
                    onAddToCart={handleAddToCart}
                    onUpdateCartQty={handleUpdateCartQty}
                    onRemoveFromCart={handleRemoveFromCart}
                    onPlaceOrder={handlePlaceOrder}
                  />
                )}
              </>
            ) : (
              <OwnerPortalView
                todayStr={TODAY_DEFAULT}
                account={activeAccount}
                patients={patients}
                visits={visits}
                vaccinations={vaccinations}
                appointments={appointments}
                cart={cart}
                orders={orders}
                onAddPatient={handleAddPatient}
                onAddAppointment={handleAddAppointment}
                onAddToCart={handleAddToCart}
                onUpdateCartQty={handleUpdateCartQty}
                onRemoveFromCart={handleRemoveFromCart}
                onPlaceOrder={handlePlaceOrder}
              />
            )}
          </main>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Account Registration & Switching Modal */}
      {authModalMode && (
        <AccountAuthModal
          accounts={accounts}
          activeAccount={activeAccount}
          initialMode={authModalMode}
          onSwitchAccount={handleSwitchAccount}
          onRegisterAccount={handleRegisterAccount}
          onClose={() => setAuthModalMode(null)}
        />
      )}

      {/* Settings & Backup Modal */}
      {showSettings && (
        <DataSettingsModal
          clinic={clinic}
          onSaveClinic={(updated) => {
            setClinic(updated);
            showToast('تم حفظ بيانات العيادة');
          }}
          onExportBackup={handleExportBackup}
          onImportBackup={handleImportBackup}
          onResetDemoData={handleResetDemoData}
          onClose={() => setShowSettings(false)}
        />
      )}

      {/* Google Drive Cloud Database Modal */}
      {showGoogleDrive && (
        <GoogleDriveModal
          currentData={{
            clinic,
            patients,
            visits,
            vaccinations,
            appointments,
            accounts,
            orders,
            cart,
          }}
          onApplyCloudData={(payload) => {
            if (payload.clinic) setClinic(payload.clinic);
            if (Array.isArray(payload.patients)) setPatients(payload.patients);
            if (Array.isArray(payload.visits)) setVisits(payload.visits);
            if (Array.isArray(payload.vaccinations))
              setVaccinations(payload.vaccinations);
            if (Array.isArray(payload.appointments))
              setAppointments(payload.appointments);
            if (Array.isArray(payload.accounts)) setAccounts(payload.accounts);
            if (Array.isArray(payload.orders)) setOrders(payload.orders);
            if (Array.isArray(payload.cart)) setCart(payload.cart);
          }}
          onNotify={showToast}
          onClose={() => setShowGoogleDrive(false)}
        />
      )}

      {/* Offline Mode Indicator */}
      <OfflineIndicator />

      {/* Toast Notification */}
      {toastMsg && (
        <div
          role="status"
          className="no-print fixed bottom-5 left-5 z-50 flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-900 px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-medium text-white shadow-lg dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800"
        >
          <Check className="h-4 w-4 text-teal-400" />
          <span>{toastMsg}</span>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import {createRoot} from 'react-dom/client';

registerSW({immediate: true});

--- FILE: src/components/AccountAuthModal.tsx ---
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  CheckCircle2,
  LogIn,
  PawPrint,
  Sprout,
  Stethoscope,
  UserPlus,
  X,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { SPECIES_LABELS } from '../data/vetData';
import { Patient, Species, UserAccount, UserRole } from '../types/vet';

interface AccountAuthModalProps {
  accounts: UserAccount[];
  activeAccount: UserAccount;
  initialMode?: 'register' | 'switch';
  onSwitchAccount: (accountId: string) => void;
  onRegisterAccount: (
    account: Omit<UserAccount, 'id' | 'createdAt'>,
    initialPatient: Omit<Patient, 'id' | 'createdAt'>
  ) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export const AccountAuthModal: React.FC<AccountAuthModalProps> = ({
  accounts,
  activeAccount,
  initialMode = 'register',
  onSwitchAccount,
  onRegisterAccount,
  onClose,
}) => {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'register' | 'switch'>(initialMode);
  const [selectedRole, setSelectedRole] = useState<
    'livestock_breeder' | 'pet_owner'
  >('livestock_breeder');

  // Shared registration fields
  const [fullName, setFullName] = useState('');
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('');
  const [city, setCity] = useState('الرياض');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');

  // Breeder-specific fields
  const [farmName, setFarmName] = useState('');
  const [herdSpecies, setHerdSpecies] = useState<Species>(Species.SheepGoat);
  const [headCount, setHeadCount] = useState('30');
  const [herdAvgWeight, setHerdAvgWeight] = useState('50');

  // Pet owner-specific fields
  const [petName, setPetName] = useState('');
  const [petSpecies, setPetSpecies] = useState<Species>(Species.Cat);
  const [petBreed, setPetBreed] = useState('');
  const [petWeight, setPetWeight] = useState('4');

  const handleRegisterSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!fullName.trim() || !phone.trim()) return;

    if (selectedRole === 'livestock_breeder') {
      const count = Math.max(1, parseInt(headCount, 10) || 1);
      const label = SPECIES_LABELS[herdSpecies];
      onRegisterAccount(
        {
          role: 'livestock_breeder',
          fullName: fullName.trim(),
          phone: phone.trim(),
          city: city.trim() || 'الرياض',
          password,
          farmName: farmName.trim() || `مزرعة ${fullName.trim()}`,
          herdTypes: label,
          headCount: count,
        },
        {
          name: `${farmName.trim() || 'قطيع'} - ${label} (${count} رأس)`,
          species: herdSpecies,
          breed: label,
          sex: 'female',
          ageText: 'متوسط القطيع',
          weightKg: Math.max(1, parseFloat(herdAvgWeight) || 50),
          identifier: `HERD-${Math.floor(100 + Math.random() * 900)}`,
          ownerName: fullName.trim(),
          ownerPhone: phone.trim(),
          allergies: 'لا يوجد',
          chronicNotes: `مزرعة: ${farmName.trim() || 'غير محددة'} · العدد: ${count} رأس`,
        }
      );
    } else {
      onRegisterAccount(
        {
          role: 'pet_owner',
          fullName: fullName.trim(),
          phone: phone.trim(),
          city: city.trim() || 'الرياض',
          password,
        },
        {
          name: petName.trim() || 'أليفي',
          species: petSpecies,
          breed: petBreed.trim() || 'غير محدد',
          sex: 'male',
          ageText: 'سنة واحدة',
          weightKg: Math.max(0.1, parseFloat(petWeight) || 4),
          identifier: `PET-${Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000)}`,
          ownerName: fullName.trim(),
          ownerPhone: phone.trim(),
          allergies: 'لا يوجد',
          chronicNotes: 'تم التسجيل عبر بوابة صاحب الحيوان الأليف',
        }
      );
    }
    onClose();
  };

  const roleBadgeInfo = (role: UserRole) => {
    if (role === 'livestock_breeder') {
      return {
        label: 'مربي مواشي',
        cardStyle:
          'border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50/60 dark:border-emerald-900/60 dark:bg-emerald-950/30',
        textStyle: 'text-emerald-800 dark:text-emerald-300',
        Icon: Sprout,
      };
    }
    if (role === 'pet_owner') {
      return {
        label: 'صاحب حيوان أليف',
        cardStyle:
          'border-yellow-300 bg-yellow-50/80 dark:border-yellow-700/60 dark:bg-yellow-950/30',
        textStyle: 'text-yellow-900 dark:text-yellow-300',
        Icon: PawPrint,
      };
    }
    return {
      label: 'طبيب بيطري',
      cardStyle:
        'border-teal-200 bg-teal-50/60 dark:border-teal-900/60 dark:bg-teal-950/30',
      textStyle: 'text-teal-800 dark:text-teal-300',
      Icon: Stethoscope,
    };
  };

  const isBreeder = selectedRole === 'livestock_breeder';

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-950/60 p-4 backdrop-blur-xs">
      <div className="max-h-[92vh] w-full max-w-2xl overflow-y-auto rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-xl dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-200 pb-4 dark:border-slate-800">
          <div>
            <h2 className="font-display text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
              الحسابات وتسجيل مستخدم جديد
            </h2>
            <p className="mt-0.5 text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
              سجّل حساب مربي مواشي (بطابع أخضر) أو صاحب حيوان أليف (بطابع أصفر ذهبي)
            </p>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="إغلاق"
            className="rounded-lg p-2 text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800"
          >
            <X className="h-5 w-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Mode Switcher Tabs */}
        <div className="mt-4 inline-flex w-full items-center gap-1 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-1 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-800/50">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setMode('register')}
            className={`flex flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold transition-colors ${
              mode === 'register'
                ? 'bg-white text-slate-900 shadow-xs dark:bg-slate-900 dark:text-white'
                : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-300'
            }`}
          >
            <UserPlus className="h-4 w-4" />
            تسجيل حساب جديد
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setMode('switch')}
            className={`flex flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold transition-colors ${
              mode === 'switch'
                ? 'bg-white text-slate-900 shadow-xs dark:bg-slate-900 dark:text-white'
                : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-300'
            }`}
          >
            <LogIn className="h-4 w-4" />
            تبديل الحساب ({accounts.length})
          </button>
        </div>

        {mode === 'register' ? (
          <form onSubmit={handleRegisterSubmit} className="mt-5 space-y-5">
            {/* Role Choice Cards */}
            <div>
              <div className="mb-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">
                1. اختر نوع الحساب
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
                {/* Green Livestock Breeder Card */}
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setSelectedRole('livestock_breeder')}
                  className={`flex flex-col items-start rounded-2xl border-2 p-4 text-right transition-all ${
                    isBreeder
                      ? 'border-emerald-600 bg-emerald-50/90 dark:border-emerald-500 dark:bg-emerald-950/40'
                      : 'border-slate-200 bg-white hover:border-emerald-300 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="flex w-full items-center justify-between">
                    <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 text-white">
                      <Sprout className="h-5 w-5" />
                    </div>
                    {isBreeder && (
                      <CheckCircle2 className="h-5 w-5 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400" />
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <div className="mt-3 font-display text-base font-bold text-emerald-950 dark:text-emerald-200">
                    مربي مواشي / مزرعة
                  </div>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-emerald-900/80 dark:text-emerald-300/80">
                    طابع أخضر لمتابعة القطيع، مواعيد التحصين الجماعي، تنبيهات سحب الحليب واللحم، وطلب زيارة الطبيب للمزرعة.
                  </p>
                </button>

                {/* Golden Yellow Pet Owner Card */}
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setSelectedRole('pet_owner')}
                  className={`flex flex-col items-start rounded-2xl border-2 p-4 text-right transition-all ${
                    !isBreeder
                      ? 'border-yellow-500 bg-yellow-50/90 dark:border-yellow-400 dark:bg-yellow-950/35'
                      : 'border-slate-200 bg-white hover:border-yellow-300 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="flex w-full items-center justify-between">
                    <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-yellow-400 text-slate-950">
                      <PawPrint className="h-5 w-5" />
                    </div>
                    {!isBreeder && (
                      <CheckCircle2 className="h-5 w-5 text-yellow-600 dark:text-yellow-400" />
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <div className="mt-3 font-display text-base font-bold text-yellow-950 dark:text-yellow-200">
                    صاحب(ة) حيوان أليف
                  </div>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-yellow-900/80 dark:text-yellow-200/80">
                    طابع أصفر ذهبي لمتابعة ملف حيوانك الأليف، تذكيرات التطعيمات، استعراض الوصفات الطبية، وحجز مواعيد العيادة.
                  </p>
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Dynamic Themed Registration Fields */}
            <div
              className={`rounded-2xl border p-5 ${
                isBreeder
                  ? 'border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50/40 dark:border-emerald-900/60 dark:bg-emerald-950/20'
                  : 'border-yellow-300 bg-yellow-50/50 dark:border-yellow-800/60 dark:bg-yellow-950/20'
              }`}
            >
              <div className="mb-3 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                2. البيانات الأساسية
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                    الاسم الكامل *
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={fullName}
                    onChange={(e) => setFullName(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder={
                      isBreeder ? 'مثال: محمد العجمي' : 'مثال: سارة الخالدي'
                    }
                    className="mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-900 focus:border-slate-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                    رقم الجوال *
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="tel"
                    required
                    value={phone}
                    onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="05XXXXXXXX"
                    className="mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3.5 py-2.5 font-mono-num text-sm text-slate-900 focus:border-slate-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                    المدينة / المنطقة
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={city}
                    onChange={(e) => setCity(e.target.value)}
                    className="mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-900 focus:border-slate-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                    كلمة المرور
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="password"
                    value={password}
                    onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="••••••••"
                    className="mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-900 focus:border-slate-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Role Specific Section */}
              <div className="mt-5 border-t border-slate-200/80 pt-4 dark:border-slate-800">
                {isBreeder ? (
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
                    <div className="sm:col-span-2">
                      <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-900 dark:text-emerald-300">
                        اسم المزرعة / العزبة
                      </label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={farmName}
                        onChange={(e) => setFarmName(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="مثال: مزرعة البركة للإنتاج الحيواني"
                        className="mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-emerald-200 bg-white px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm dark:border-emerald-800 dark:bg-slate-900"
                      />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-900 dark:text-emerald-300">
                        نوع المواشي الأساسي
                      </label>
                      <select
                        value={herdSpecies}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                          setHerdSpecies(e.target.value as Species)
                        }
                        className="mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-emerald-200 bg-white px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm dark:border-emerald-800 dark:bg-slate-900"
                      >
                        <option value={Species.SheepGoat}>أغنام وماعز</option>
                        <option value={Species.Cattle}>أبقار</option>
                        <option value={Species.Camel}>إبل</option>
                        <option value={Species.Horse}>خيول</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                      <div>
                        <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-900 dark:text-emerald-300">
                          عدد الرؤوس
                        </label>
                        <input
                          type="number"
                          min="1"
                          value={headCount}
                          onChange={(e) => setHeadCount(e.target.value)}
                          className="mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-emerald-200 bg-white px-3 py-2.5 font-mono-num text-sm dark:border-emerald-800 dark:bg-slate-900"
                        />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-900 dark:text-emerald-300">
                          متوسط الوزن (كغ)
                        </label>
                        <input
                          type="number"
                          min="5"
                          value={herdAvgWeight}
                          onChange={(e) => setHerdAvgWeight(e.target.value)}
                          className="mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-emerald-200 bg-white px-3 py-2.5 font-mono-num text-sm dark:border-emerald-800 dark:bg-slate-900"
                        />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-yellow-950 dark:text-yellow-300">
                        اسم الحيوان الأليف *
                      </label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        required
                        value={petName}
                        onChange={(e) => setPetName(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="مثال: مشمش / كوكي"
                        className="mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-yellow-300 bg-white px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm dark:border-yellow-700 dark:bg-slate-900"
                      />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-yellow-950 dark:text-yellow-300">
                        النوع
                      </label>
                      <select
                        value={petSpecies}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                          setPetSpecies(e.target.value as Species)
                        }
                        className="mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-yellow-300 bg-white px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm dark:border-yellow-700 dark:bg-slate-900"
                      >
                        <option value={Species.Cat}>قط</option>
                        <option value={Species.Dog}>كلب</option>
                        <option value={Species.Bird}>طائر</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-yellow-950 dark:text-yellow-300">
                        السلالة
                      </label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={petBreed}
                        onChange={(e) => setPetBreed(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="مثال: شيرازي / جولدن ريتريفر"
                        className="mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-yellow-300 bg-white px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm dark:border-yellow-700 dark:bg-slate-900"
                      />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-yellow-950 dark:text-yellow-300">
                        الوزن التقريبي (كغ)
                      </label>
                      <input
                        type="number"
                        step="0.1"
                        min="0.1"
                        value={petWeight}
                        onChange={(e) => setPetWeight(e.target.value)}
                        className="mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-yellow-300 bg-white px-3.5 py-2.5 font-mono-num text-sm dark:border-yellow-700 dark:bg-slate-900"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-3">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onClose}
                className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:border-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300"
              >
                إلغاء
              </button>
              <button
                type="submit"
                className={`rounded-xl px-6 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold transition-colors ${
                  isBreeder
                    ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white hover:bg-emerald-700'
                    : 'bg-yellow-400 text-slate-950 hover:bg-yellow-500'
                }`}
              >
                {isBreeder
                  ? 'إنشاء حساب مربي مواشي'
                  : 'إنشاء حساب صاحب حيوان أليف'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        ) : (
          <div className="mt-5 space-y-3">
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
              اختر أي حساب لتجربة الواجهة الخاصة به (الطبيب البيطري، مربي المواشي الأخضر، أو صاحب الحيوان الأليف الأصفر الذهبي):
            </p>
            <div className="space-y-2.5">
              {accounts.map((acc) => {
                const info = roleBadgeInfo(acc.role);
                const Icon = info.Icon;
                const isCurrent = acc.id === activeAccount.id;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={acc.id}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      onSwitchAccount(acc.id);
                      onClose();
                    }}
                    className={`flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-4 rounded-2xl border p-4 text-right transition-all ${
                      isCurrent
                        ? `${info.cardStyle} ring-2 ring-slate-900/10 dark:ring-white/20`
                        : 'border-slate-200 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:hover:bg-slate-800/50'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3.5">
                      <div
                        className={`flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl ${
                          acc.role === 'livestock_breeder'
                            ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white'
                            : acc.role === 'pet_owner'
                            ? 'bg-yellow-400 text-slate-950'
                            : 'bg-teal-600 text-white'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <Icon className="h-5 w-5" />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <div className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                          {acc.fullName}
                        </div>
                        <div className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                          <span className={`font-semibold ${info.textStyle}`}>
                            {info.label}
                          </span>
                          {' · '}
                          <span className="font-mono-num">{acc.phone}</span>
                          {acc.farmName ? ` · ${acc.farmName}` : ''}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <span className="whitespace-nowrap text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                      {isCurrent ? 'الحساب النشط' : 'دخول'}
                    </span>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

--- FILE: src/components/CalculatorsTab.tsx ---
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import {
  AlertTriangle,
  Baby,
  Calculator,
  Copy,
  Droplets,
  Search,
  ShieldAlert,
} from 'lucide-react';
import {
  DRUG_CATEGORY_LABELS,
  SPECIES_LABELS,
  SPECIES_PHYSIOLOGY,
  VETERINARY_FORMULARY,
} from '../data/vetData';
import { DrugFormularyItem, Patient, Species } from '../types/vet';

interface CalculatorsTabProps {
  patients: Patient[];
  preselectedPatientId?: string | null;
  onCopyText: (text: string) => void;
}

type CalcMode = 'dosage' | 'fluids' | 'gestation';

export const CalculatorsTab: React.FC<CalculatorsTabProps> = ({
  patients,
  preselectedPatientId,
  onCopyText,
}) => {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<CalcMode>('dosage');

  // Shared Patient / Weight state
  const [selectedPatientId, setSelectedPatientId] = useState<string>(
    preselectedPatientId || ''
  );
  const [species, setSpecies] = useState<Species>(Species.Dog);
  const [weightKg, setWeightKg] = useState<number>(20);

  // Dosage Calculator State
  const [drugSearch, setDrugSearch] = useState('');
  const [categoryFilter, setCategoryFilter] = useState<string>('all');
  const [selectedDrugId, setSelectedDrugId] = useState<string>(
    VETERINARY_FORMULARY[0].id
  );
  const [customDoseMgKg, setCustomDoseMgKg] = useState<number>(12.5);
  const [concentration, setConcentration] = useState<number>(
    VETERINARY_FORMULARY[0].defaultConcentrationMgPerUnit
  );

  // Fluid Calculator State
  const [dehydrationPct, setDehydrationPct] = useState<number>(5);
  const [ongoingLossMl, setOngoingLossMl] = useState<number>(0);
  const [dripFactor, setDripFactor] = useState<15 | 20 | 60>(20);

  // Gestation Calculator State
  const [gestationSpecies, setGestationSpecies] = useState<Species>(Species.Cattle);
  const [matingDate, setMatingDate] = useState<string>('2026-10-01');

  useEffect(() => {
    if (preselectedPatientId) {
      const p = patients.find((item) => item.id === preselectedPatientId);
      if (p) {
        setSelectedPatientId(p.id);
        setSpecies(p.species);
        setWeightKg(p.weightKg);
        setMode('dosage');
      }
    }
  }, [preselectedPatientId, patients]);

  const handleSelectPatient = (id: string) => {
    setSelectedPatientId(id);
    if (!id) return;
    const p = patients.find((item) => item.id === id);
    if (p) {
      setSpecies(p.species);
      setWeightKg(p.weightKg);
    }
  };

  const compatibleDrugs = useMemo(() => {
    return VETERINARY_FORMULARY.filter((drug) => {
      const matchesSpecies = Boolean(drug.speciesDoses[species]);
      const matchesCategory =
        categoryFilter === 'all' || drug.category === categoryFilter;
      const q = drugSearch.trim().toLowerCase();
      const matchesSearch =
        !q ||
        drug.nameAr.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        drug.nameEn.toLowerCase().includes(q);
      return matchesSpecies && matchesCategory && matchesSearch;
    });
  }, [species, categoryFilter, drugSearch]);

  const activeDrug: DrugFormularyItem | undefined = useMemo(() => {
    const found = compatibleDrugs.find((d) => d.id === selectedDrugId);
    return found || compatibleDrugs[0];
  }, [compatibleDrugs, selectedDrugId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeDrug) {
      const spec = activeDrug.speciesDoses[species];
      if (spec) {
        setCustomDoseMgKg(spec.defaultMgKg);
      }
      setConcentration(activeDrug.defaultConcentrationMgPerUnit);
    }
  }, [activeDrug?.id, species]);

  const activeDoseSpec = activeDrug?.speciesDoses[species];

  // Dosage Math
  const safeWeight = Math.max(0.05, Number(weightKg) || 0);
  const safeDose = Math.max(0, Number(customDoseMgKg) || 0);
  const safeConc = Math.max(0.01, Number(concentration) || 1);
  const totalMg = safeWeight * safeDose;
  const totalUnits = totalMg / safeConc;

  const isOutOfRange =
    activeDoseSpec &&
    (safeDose < activeDoseSpec.minMgKg || safeDose > activeDoseSpec.maxMgKg);

  // Fluid Therapy Math
  const maintenanceRate =
    SPECIES_PHYSIOLOGY[species]?.maintenanceFluidMlKgDay ?? 50;
  const maintenance24h = safeWeight * maintenanceRate;
  const deficitMl = safeWeight * dehydrationPct * 10; // Weight(kg) * %Dehydration * 10 = mL
  const totalFluid24h = maintenance24h + deficitMl + (Number(ongoingLossMl) || 0);
  const hourlyRateMl = totalFluid24h / 24;
  const dropsPerMin = (hourlyRateMl * dripFactor) / 60;

  // Gestation Math
  const gestationDays = SPECIES_PHYSIOLOGY[gestationSpecies]?.gestationDays ?? 63;
  const gestationResult = useMemo(() => {
    if (!matingDate) return null;
    const start = new Date(matingDate + 'T00:00:00');
    if (Number.isNaN(start.getTime())) return null;
    const due = new Date(start);
    due.setDate(due.getDate() + gestationDays);
    const earliest = new Date(start);
    earliest.setDate(earliest.getDate() + Math.max(1, gestationDays - 4));
    const latest = new Date(start);
    latest.setDate(latest.getDate() + gestationDays + 4);
    const fmt = (d: Date) => d.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    return {
      due: fmt(due),
      window: `${fmt(earliest)} إلى ${fmt(latest)}`,
    };
  }, [matingDate, gestationDays]);

  const handleCopyPrescription = () => {
    if (!activeDrug || !activeDoseSpec) return;
    const patientObj = patients.find((p) => p.id === selectedPatientId);
    const patientLabel = patientObj
      ? `المريض: ${patientObj.name} (${SPECIES_LABELS[species]} - ${safeWeight} كغ)`
      : `النوع: ${SPECIES_LABELS[species]} - الوزن: ${safeWeight} كغ`;
    const text = `${patientLabel}\nالدواء: ${activeDrug.nameAr} (${activeDrug.nameEn})\nالجرعة: ${safeDose} ملغ/كغ = ${totalMg.toFixed(
      1
    )} ملغ (${totalUnits.toFixed(2)} ${activeDrug.unitLabel} بتركيز ${safeConc} ملغ/${
      activeDrug.unitLabel
    })\nطريقة الإعطاء: ${activeDoseSpec.routes[0]} · التكرار: ${activeDoseSpec.frequency}`;
    onCopyText(text);
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Mode Switcher */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-1 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setMode('dosage')}
            className={`inline-flex items-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-xs font-medium transition-colors ${
              mode === 'dosage'
                ? 'bg-teal-600 text-white'
                : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-300 dark:hover:text-white'
            }`}
          >
            <Calculator className="h-4 w-4" />
            حاسبة الجرعات الدوائية
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setMode('fluids')}
            className={`inline-flex items-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-xs font-medium transition-colors ${
              mode === 'fluids'
                ? 'bg-teal-600 text-white'
                : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-300 dark:hover:text-white'
            }`}
          >
            <Droplets className="h-4 w-4" />
            السوائل الوريدية ومعدل التقطير
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setMode('gestation')}
            className={`inline-flex items-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-xs font-medium transition-colors ${
              mode === 'gestation'
                ? 'bg-teal-600 text-white'
                : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-300 dark:hover:text-white'
            }`}
          >
            <Baby className="h-4 w-4" />
            حاسبة موعد الولادة المتوقع
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Quick Patient Auto-Fill */}
        {mode !== 'gestation' && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <label
              htmlFor="calc-patient-select"
              className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300"
            >
              تعبئة تلقائية من مريض:
            </label>
            <select
              id="calc-patient-select"
              value={selectedPatientId}
              onChange={(e) => handleSelectPatient(e.target.value)}
              className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:border-teal-600 focus:outline-none dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100"
            >
              <option value="">إدخال يدوي حر</option>
              {patients.map((p) => (
                <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>
                  {p.name} ({SPECIES_LABELS[p.species]} - {p.weightKg} كغ)
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Mode 1: Drug Dosage Calculator */}
      {mode === 'dosage' && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 lg:grid-cols-12">
          {/* Left Column: Patient Params & Drug Picker */}
          <div className="space-y-5 lg:col-span-5">
            <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900">
              <h3 className="font-display text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
                1. بيانات الحيوان والوزن
              </h3>
              <div className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
                    الفصيلة / النوع
                  </label>
                  <select
                    value={species}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      setSelectedPatientId('');
                      setSpecies(e.target.value as Species);
                    }}
                    className="mt-1.5 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-900 focus:border-teal-600 focus:outline-none dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100"
                  >
                    {Object.entries(SPECIES_LABELS).map(([k, label]) => (
                      <option key={k} value={k}>
                        {label}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
                    الوزن (كغ)
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    min="0.05"
                    step="0.1"
                    value={weightKg}
                    onChange={(e) => setWeightKg(parseFloat(e.target.value) || 0)}
                    className="mt-1.5 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 font-mono-num text-sm text-slate-900 focus:border-teal-600 focus:outline-none dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900">
              <h3 className="font-display text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
                2. اختيار الدواء من الدليل البيطري
              </h3>
              <div className="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-2 sm:flex-row">
                <div className="relative flex-1">
                  <Search className="pointer-events-none absolute right-3 top-2.5 h-4 w-4 text-slate-400" />
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={drugSearch}
                    onChange={(e) => setDrugSearch(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="ابحث بالعربي أو الإنجليزي..."
                    className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white py-2 pr-9 pl-3 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:border-teal-600 focus:outline-none dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100"
                  />
                </div>
                <select
                  value={categoryFilter}
                  onChange={(e) => setCategoryFilter(e.target.value)}
                  className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-800 focus:border-teal-600 focus:outline-none dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100"
                >
                  <option value="all">كل التصنيفات</option>
                  {Object.entries(DRUG_CATEGORY_LABELS).map(([k, label]) => (
                    <option key={k} value={k}>
                      {label}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>

              <div className="mt-3 max-h-80 divide-y divide-slate-100 overflow-y-auto rounded-lg border border-slate-200 dark:divide-slate-800 dark:border-slate-800">
                {compatibleDrugs.length === 0 ? (
                  <div className="p-6 text-center text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                    لا توجد أدوية مطابقة لـ ({SPECIES_LABELS[species]}) في هذا البحث.
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  compatibleDrugs.map((drug) => {
                    const isActive = activeDrug?.id === drug.id;
                    const spec = drug.speciesDoses[species];
                    return (
                      <button
                        key={drug.id}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setSelectedDrugId(drug.id)}
                        className={`flex w-full items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 text-right transition-colors ${
                          isActive
                            ? 'bg-teal-50/80 dark:bg-teal-950/40'
                            : 'hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800/50'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <div>
                          <div
                            className={`text-sm font-medium ${
                              isActive
                                ? 'text-teal-800 dark:text-teal-300'
                                : 'text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100'
                            }`}
                          >
                            {drug.nameAr}
                          </div>
                          <div className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                            {drug.nameEn} · {DRUG_CATEGORY_LABELS[drug.category]}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                        {spec && (
                          <span className="font-mono-num text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
                            {spec.defaultMgKg} mg/kg
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </button>
                    );
                  })
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Right Column: Live Calculation & Safety Guidance */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-7">
            {activeDrug && activeDoseSpec ? (
              <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-4 border-b border-slate-200 pb-5 dark:border-slate-800">
                  <div>
                    <div className="text-xs font-medium text-teal-700 dark:text-teal-400">
                      {DRUG_CATEGORY_LABELS[activeDrug.category]} · {SPECIES_LABELS[species]}
                    </div>
                    <h2 className="mt-1 font-display text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
                      {activeDrug.nameAr}
                    </h2>
                    <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                      {activeDrug.nameEn}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={handleCopyPrescription}
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg bg-teal-600 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-white transition-colors hover:bg-teal-700"
                  >
                    <Copy className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                    نسخ سطر الوصفة
                  </button>
                </div>

                {/* Calculated Output Highlight */}
                <div className="mt-6 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
                  <div className="rounded-xl border border-teal-200 bg-teal-50/50 p-4 dark:border-teal-900/60 dark:bg-teal-950/30">
                    <div className="text-xs font-medium text-teal-800 dark:text-teal-300">
                      الكمية المطلوب إعطاؤها
                    </div>
                    <div className="mt-2 font-mono-num text-3xl font-bold text-teal-700 dark:text-teal-300">
                      {totalUnits.toFixed(2)}{' '}
                      <span className="text-base font-medium">{activeDrug.unitLabel}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-teal-700/80 dark:text-teal-400/80">
                      لكل جرعة واحدة
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/70 p-4 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-800/40">
                    <div className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                      إجمالي المادة الفعالة
                    </div>
                    <div className="mt-2 font-mono-num text-2xl font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                      {totalMg.toFixed(1)}{' '}
                      <span className="text-sm font-normal">ملغ</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="mt-1 font-mono-num text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                      {safeWeight} كغ × {safeDose} ملغ/كغ
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/70 p-4 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-800/40">
                    <div className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                      طرق الإعطاء الموصى بها
                    </div>
                    <div className="mt-2 font-mono-num text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                      {activeDoseSpec.routes.join(' / ')}
                    </div>
                    <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
