                      {activeDoseSpec.frequency}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Adjustable Dose & Concentration Controls */}
                <div className="mt-6 grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 border-t border-slate-200 pt-5 sm:grid-cols-2 dark:border-slate-800">
                  <div>
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                      <label className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                        الجرعة المختارة (ملغ / كغ)
                      </label>
                      <span className="font-mono-num text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                        المدى: {activeDoseSpec.minMgKg} – {activeDoseSpec.maxMgKg}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <input
                      type="number"
                      step="0.05"
                      min="0.01"
                      value={customDoseMgKg}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        setCustomDoseMgKg(parseFloat(e.target.value) || 0)
                      }
                      className="mt-2 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 font-mono-num text-sm text-slate-900 focus:border-teal-600 focus:outline-none dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100"
                    />
                    {isOutOfRange && (
                      <div className="mt-1.5 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-400">
                        <AlertTriangle className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
                        الجرعة المدخلة خارج المدى المرجعي القياسي لهذا النوع.
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                      <label className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                        تركيز المستحضر (ملغ / {activeDrug.unitLabel})
                      </label>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() =>
                          setConcentration(activeDrug.defaultConcentrationMgPerUnit)
                        }
                        className="text-xs text-teal-700 hover:underline dark:text-teal-400"
                      >
                        التركيز القياسي ({activeDrug.defaultConcentrationMgPerUnit})
                      </button>
                    </div>
                    <input
                      type="number"
                      step="0.5"
                      min="0.1"
                      value={concentration}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        setConcentration(parseFloat(e.target.value) || 1)
                      }
                      className="mt-2 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 font-mono-num text-sm text-slate-900 focus:border-teal-600 focus:outline-none dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Livestock Withdrawal Period (if applicable) */}
                {(activeDoseSpec.meatWithdrawalDays !== undefined ||
                  activeDoseSpec.milkWithdrawalDays !== undefined) && (
                  <div className="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50/60 p-4 dark:border-amber-900/50 dark:bg-amber-950/20">
                    <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-amber-900 dark:text-amber-300">
                      فترة السحب للحيوانات الإنتاجية (Withdrawal Period)
                    </div>
                    <div className="mt-1.5 flex flex-wrap gap-6 text-xs text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-200">
                      {activeDoseSpec.meatWithdrawalDays !== undefined && (
                        <span>
                          اللحم:{' '}
                          <strong className="font-mono-num">
                            {activeDoseSpec.meatWithdrawalDays} يوماً
                          </strong>
                        </span>
                      )}
                      {activeDoseSpec.milkWithdrawalDays !== undefined && (
                        <span>
                          الحليب:{' '}
                          <strong className="font-mono-num">
                            {activeDoseSpec.milkWithdrawalDays} يوماً
                          </strong>
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* Contraindications & Clinical Notes */}
                <div className="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50/50 p-4 dark:border-rose-900/50 dark:bg-rose-950/20">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold text-rose-800 dark:text-rose-300">
                    <ShieldAlert className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
                    موانع الاستخدام والتحذيرات السريرية
                  </div>
                  <p className="mt-1.5 text-xs leading-relaxed text-rose-900/90 dark:text-rose-200">
                    {activeDrug.contraindications}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-10 text-center text-sm text-slate-500 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:text-slate-400">
                اختر دواءً متوافقاً مع الفصيلة لعرض الجرعة المحسوبة والتحذيرات.
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Mode 2: IV Fluid Therapy & Drip Rate */}
      {mode === 'fluids' && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 lg:grid-cols-12">
          <div className="space-y-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 lg:col-span-5 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900">
            <h3 className="font-display text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
              معطيات الجفاف والتروية الوريدية
            </h3>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
                  النوع
                </label>
                <select
                  value={species}
                  onChange={(e) => setSpecies(e.target.value as Species)}
                  className="mt-1.5 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-900 focus:border-teal-600 focus:outline-none dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100"
                >
                  {Object.entries(SPECIES_LABELS).map(([k, label]) => (
                    <option key={k} value={k}>
                      {label}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
                  الوزن (كغ)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  min="0.1"
                  step="0.5"
                  value={weightKg}
                  onChange={(e) => setWeightKg(parseFloat(e.target.value) || 0)}
                  className="mt-1.5 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 font-mono-num text-sm text-slate-900 focus:border-teal-600 focus:outline-none dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
                <span>نسبة الجفاف التقديرية (%)</span>
                <span className="font-mono-num font-semibold text-teal-700 dark:text-teal-400">
                  {dehydrationPct}%
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="mt-2 grid grid-cols-5 gap-1.5">
                {[0, 5, 7, 10, 12].map((pct) => (
                  <button
                    key={pct}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setDehydrationPct(pct)}
                    className={`rounded-lg border py-2 font-mono-num text-xs font-medium transition-colors ${
                      dehydrationPct === pct
                        ? 'border-teal-600 bg-teal-600 text-white'
                        : 'border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-300'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {pct}%
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
              <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                5%: جفاف طفيف (لزوجة الأغشية) · 7-8%: تأخر عودة طية الجلد · 10-12%: غؤور العين وصدمة.
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
                  فاقد مستمر إضافي (مل/يوم)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  min="0"
                  step="50"
                  value={ongoingLossMl}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setOngoingLossMl(parseFloat(e.target.value) || 0)
                  }
                  className="mt-1.5 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 font-mono-num text-sm text-slate-900 focus:border-teal-600 focus:outline-none dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
                  معامل جهاز التقطير (Drip Set)
                </label>
                <select
                  value={dripFactor}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setDripFactor(Number(e.target.value) as 15 | 20 | 60)
                  }
                  className="mt-1.5 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 font-mono-num text-sm text-slate-900 focus:border-teal-600 focus:outline-none dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100"
                >
                  <option value={20}>20 قطرة/مل (قياسي)</option>
                  <option value={15}>15 قطرة/مل (كبير)</option>
                  <option value={60}>60 قطرة/مل (مايكرو للقطط/الجراء)</option>
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 lg:col-span-7 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900">
            <h3 className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
              خطة السوائل الوريدية خلال 24 ساعة
            </h3>

            <div className="mt-5 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
              <div className="rounded-xl border border-teal-200 bg-teal-50/50 p-4 dark:border-teal-900/60 dark:bg-teal-950/30">
                <div className="text-xs font-medium text-teal-800 dark:text-teal-300">
                  معدل الضخ بالساعة
                </div>
                <div className="mt-2 font-mono-num text-3xl font-bold text-teal-700 dark:text-teal-300">
                  {hourlyRateMl.toFixed(1)}{' '}
                  <span className="text-sm font-normal">مل/ساعة</span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/70 p-4 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-800/40">
                <div className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                  معدل التقطير اليدوي
                </div>
                <div className="mt-2 font-mono-num text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                  {Math.round(dropsPerMin)}{' '}
                  <span className="text-sm font-normal">قطرة/دقيقة</span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/70 p-4 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-800/40">
                <div className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                  إجمالي السوائل (24 ساعة)
                </div>
                <div className="mt-2 font-mono-num text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                  {Math.round(totalFluid24h)}{' '}
                  <span className="text-sm font-normal">مل</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-6 divide-y divide-slate-200 border-t border-slate-200 text-sm dark:divide-slate-800 dark:border-slate-800">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between py-3">
                <span className="text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
                  حجم تعويض الجفاف (الوزن × {dehydrationPct}% × 10)
                </span>
                <span className="font-mono-num font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                  {Math.round(deficitMl)} مل
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between py-3">
                <span className="text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
                  حجم الصيانة اليومي ({maintenanceRate} مل/كغ/يوم)
                </span>
                <span className="font-mono-num font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                  {Math.round(maintenance24h)} مل
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between py-3">
                <span className="text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
                  الفاقد المستمر (قيء / إسهال)
                </span>
                <span className="font-mono-num font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                  {Math.round(ongoingLossMl)} مل
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Mode 3: Gestation Due Date Calculator */}
      {mode === 'gestation' && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 lg:grid-cols-12">
          <div className="space-y-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 lg:col-span-5 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900">
            <h3 className="font-display text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
              حاسبة موعد الولادة المتوقع
            </h3>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
                النوع الحيواني
              </label>
              <select
                value={gestationSpecies}
                onChange={(e) => setGestationSpecies(e.target.value as Species)}
                className="mt-1.5 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-900 focus:border-teal-600 focus:outline-none dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100"
              >
                {Object.entries(SPECIES_LABELS)
                  .filter(([k]) => k !== Species.Bird)
                  .map(([k, label]) => (
                    <option key={k} value={k}>
                      {label} ({SPECIES_PHYSIOLOGY[k as Species].gestationDays} يوم)
                    </option>
                  ))}
              </select>
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
                تاريخ التلقيح / التزاوج
              </label>
              <input
                type="date"
                value={matingDate}
                onChange={(e) => setMatingDate(e.target.value)}
                className="mt-1.5 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 font-mono-num text-sm text-slate-900 focus:border-teal-600 focus:outline-none dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 lg:col-span-7 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900">
            <h3 className="font-display text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
              النتيجة التناسلية المتوقعة
            </h3>
            {gestationResult ? (
              <div className="mt-5 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
                <div className="rounded-xl border border-teal-200 bg-teal-50/50 p-5 dark:border-teal-900/60 dark:bg-teal-950/30">
                  <div className="text-xs font-medium text-teal-800 dark:text-teal-300">
                    التاريخ المتوقع للولادة
                  </div>
                  <div className="mt-2 font-mono-num text-2xl font-bold text-teal-700 dark:text-teal-300">
                    {gestationResult.due}
                  </div>
                  <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
                    متوسط مدة الحمل: {gestationDays} يوماً
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/70 p-5 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-800/40">
                  <div className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                    النطاق الطبيعي الآمن (±4 أيام)
                  </div>
                  <div className="mt-2 font-mono-num text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                    {gestationResult.window}
                  </div>
                  <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                    يُنصح بالفحص بالسونار في الثلث الأول
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <p className="mt-4 text-xs text-slate-500">يرجى إدخال تاريخ تلقيح صحيح.</p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

--- FILE: src/components/DataSettingsModal.tsx ---
import React, { useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Download, RotateCcw, Upload, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { ClinicProfile } from '../types/vet';

interface DataSettingsModalProps {
  clinic: ClinicProfile;
  onSaveClinic: (clinic: ClinicProfile) => void;
  onExportBackup: () => void;
  onImportBackup: (jsonText: string) => void;
  onResetDemoData: () => void;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export const DataSettingsModal: React.FC<DataSettingsModalProps> = ({
  clinic,
  onSaveClinic,
  onExportBackup,
  onImportBackup,
  onResetDemoData,
  onClose,
}) => {
  const [form, setForm] = useState<ClinicProfile>(clinic);
  const [confirmReset, setConfirmReset] = useState(false);
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const handleSave = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    onSaveClinic(form);
    onClose();
  };

  const handleFileUpload = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => {
      if (typeof reader.result === 'string') {
        onImportBackup(reader.result);
      }
    };
    reader.readAsText(file);
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-950/60 p-4 backdrop-blur-xs">
      <div className="max-h-[90vh] w-full max-w-lg overflow-y-auto rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-xl dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-200 pb-3 dark:border-slate-800">
          <h3 className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
            إعدادات العيادة والنسخ الاحتياطي
          </h3>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="rounded-lg p-1.5 text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800"
          >
            <X className="h-4 w-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSave} className="mt-4 space-y-4">
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
              اسم العيادة / المركز البيطري
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              required
              value={form.clinicName}
              onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, clinicName: e.target.value })}
              className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-xs dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800"
            />
          </div>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                اسم الطبيب البيطري
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                required
                value={form.doctorName}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  setForm({ ...form, doctorName: e.target.value })
                }
                className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-xs dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800"
              />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                رقم الترخيص المهني
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={form.licenseNumber}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  setForm({ ...form, licenseNumber: e.target.value })
                }
                className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 font-mono-num text-xs dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800"
              />
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                المدينة
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={form.city}
                onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, city: e.target.value })}
                className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-xs dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800"
              />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                رقم الهاتف
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={form.phone}
                onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, phone: e.target.value })}
                className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 font-mono-num text-xs dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex justify-end">
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="rounded-lg bg-teal-600 px-5 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-white hover:bg-teal-700"
            >
              حفظ بيانات العيادة
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>

        <div className="mt-6 border-t border-slate-200 pt-5 dark:border-slate-800">
          <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">
            إدارة بيانات العيادة والنسخ الاحتياطي (JSON)
          </div>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
            يتم حفظ جميع المرضى والسجلات محلياً على جهازك تلقائياً. يمكنك تصدير نسخة احتياطية في أي وقت.
          </p>

          <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onExportBackup}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:border-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 dark:hover:bg-slate-800"
            >
              <Download className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              تصدير نسخة احتياطية
            </button>

            <input
              ref={fileInputRef}
              type="file"
              accept="application/json,.json"
              onChange={handleFileUpload}
              className="hidden"
            />
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:border-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 dark:hover:bg-slate-800"
            >
              <Upload className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              استيراد نسخة سابقة
            </button>

            {!confirmReset ? (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setConfirmReset(true)}
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-rose-200 px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-rose-700 hover:bg-rose-50 dark:border-rose-900/50 dark:text-rose-400 dark:hover:bg-rose-950/30"
              >
                <RotateCcw className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                استعادة البيانات الافتراضية
              </button>
            ) : (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  onResetDemoData();
                  setConfirmReset(false);
                  onClose();
                }}
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg bg-rose-600 px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-white hover:bg-rose-700"
              >
                تأكيد مسح واستعادة الافتراضي
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

--- FILE: src/components/GoogleDriveModal.tsx ---
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { User } from 'firebase/auth';
import {
  AlertTriangle,
  CheckCircle2,
  Cloud,
  CloudDownload,
  CloudUpload,
  Download,
  ExternalLink,
  FileCode2,
  FolderOpen,
  LogOut,
  RefreshCw,
  Trash2,
  X,
} from 'lucide-react';
import {
  buildCompleteDatabasePayload,
  CLOUD_DB_FILENAME,
  CLOUD_SOURCE_TXT_FILENAME,
  CLOUD_SOURCE_ZIP_FILENAME,
  CloudDatabasePayload,
  deleteCloudDriveFiles,
  DriveFileMeta,
  getAccessToken,
  googleSignIn,
  initAuth,
  listCloudWorkspaceFiles,
  logoutGoogle,
  readCloudDatabaseFile,
  transferFullDatabaseAndCodeToDrive,
} from '../services/googleDriveDb';

interface GoogleDriveModalProps {
  currentData: Omit<
    CloudDatabasePayload,
    'version' | 'updatedAt' | 'referenceCatalogs'
  >;
  onApplyCloudData: (payload: CloudDatabasePayload) => void;
  onNotify: (msg: string) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
}

type ConfirmAction =
  | { type: 'transfer_all'; isOverwrite: boolean }
  | { type: 'restore_db'; file: DriveFileMeta }
  | { type: 'delete_all'; files: DriveFileMeta[] }
  | null;

export const GoogleDriveModal: React.FC<GoogleDriveModalProps> = ({
  currentData,
  onApplyCloudData,
  onNotify,
  onClose,
}) => {
  const [needsAuth, setNeedsAuth] = useState<boolean>(!getAccessToken());
  const [googleUser, setGoogleUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  const [isLoggingIn, setIsLoggingIn] = useState(false);
  const [isBusy, setIsBusy] = useState(false);
  const [progressStep, setProgressStep] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [cloudFolder, setCloudFolder] = useState<DriveFileMeta | null>(null);
  const [cloudFiles, setCloudFiles] = useState<DriveFileMeta[]>([]);
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [confirmAction, setConfirmAction] = useState<ConfirmAction>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const unsub = initAuth(
      (user) => {
        setGoogleUser(user);
        setNeedsAuth(false);
      },
      () => {
        setGoogleUser(null);
        setNeedsAuth(true);
      }
    );
    return () => unsub();
  }, []);

  const refreshCloudStatus = async () => {
    setErrorMsg(null);
    setIsBusy(true);
    try {
      const res = await listCloudWorkspaceFiles();
      setCloudFolder(res.folder);
      setCloudFiles(res.files);
    } catch (err) {
      const msg =
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'تعذر الاتصال بـ Google Drive';
      setErrorMsg(msg);
      if (msg.includes('401') || msg.includes('انتهت')) {
        setNeedsAuth(true);
      }
    } finally {
      setIsBusy(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!needsAuth && getAccessToken()) {
      void refreshCloudStatus();
    }
  }, [needsAuth]);

  const handleLogin = async () => {
    setErrorMsg(null);
    setIsLoggingIn(true);
    try {
      const result = await googleSignIn();
      setGoogleUser(result.user);
      setNeedsAuth(false);
      onNotify('تم تسجيل الدخول بـ Google بنجاح — يمكنك الآن نقل كل شيء');
    } catch (err) {
      setErrorMsg(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'فشل تسجيل الدخول بحساب Google'
      );
    } finally {
      setIsLoggingIn(false);
    }
  };

  const handleLogout = async () => {
    await logoutGoogle();
    setGoogleUser(null);
    setCloudFolder(null);
    setCloudFiles([]);
    setNeedsAuth(true);
    onNotify('تم فصل الاتصال بـ Google Drive');
  };

  const handleDownloadLocalDb = () => {
    const payload = buildCompleteDatabasePayload(currentData);
    const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2)], {
      type: 'application/json',
    });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = CLOUD_DB_FILENAME;
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    onNotify('تم تنزيل ملف قاعدة البيانات الكاملة على جهازك');
  };

  const executeConfirmedAction = async () => {
    if (!confirmAction) return;
    const action = confirmAction;
    setConfirmAction(null);
    setErrorMsg(null);
    setIsBusy(true);

    try {
      if (action.type === 'transfer_all') {
        const payload = buildCompleteDatabasePayload(currentData);
        const result = await transferFullDatabaseAndCodeToDrive(
          payload,
          cloudFiles,
          (step) => setProgressStep(step)
        );
        setCloudFolder(result.folder);
        await refreshCloudStatus();
        onNotify(
          `تم نقل قاعدة البيانات وجميع الأكواد (${result.uploaded.length} ملفات) إلى Google Drive`
        );
      } else if (action.type === 'restore_db') {
        setProgressStep('جاري قراءة قاعدة البيانات من Google Drive...');
        const payload = await readCloudDatabaseFile(action.file.id);
        onApplyCloudData(payload);
        onNotify('تمت استعادة قاعدة البيانات من Google Drive بنجاح');
      } else if (action.type === 'delete_all') {
        setProgressStep('جاري حذف الملفات المحددة من Google Drive...');
        await deleteCloudDriveFiles(action.files.map((f) => f.id));
        setCloudFiles([]);
        onNotify('تم حذف الملفات من Google Drive');
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setErrorMsg(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'حدث خطأ أثناء تنفيذ العملية'
      );
    } finally {
      setProgressStep(null);
      setIsBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const dbFile = cloudFiles.find((f) => f.name === CLOUD_DB_FILENAME);

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-950/60 p-4 backdrop-blur-xs">
      <div className="max-h-[92vh] w-full max-w-2xl overflow-y-auto rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-xl dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-200 pb-4 dark:border-slate-800">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-teal-50 text-teal-700 dark:bg-teal-950/60 dark:text-teal-300">
              <Cloud className="h-5 w-5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h2 className="font-display text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
                نقل قاعدة البيانات وجميع الأكواد إلى Google Drive
              </h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                ارفع قاعدة بيانات العيادة + ملف الكود البرمجي الكامل في مجلد واحد بحسابك
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="إغلاق"
            className="rounded-lg p-1.5 text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800"
          >
            <X className="h-4 w-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Error Message */}
        {errorMsg && (
          <div className="mt-4 flex items-start gap-2 rounded-xl border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50 p-3 text-xs text-rose-800 dark:border-rose-900/50 dark:bg-rose-950/30 dark:text-rose-200">
            <AlertTriangle className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
            <span>{errorMsg}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Progress Indicator */}
        {progressStep && (
          <div className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-xl border border-teal-200 bg-teal-50 p-3 text-xs font-semibold text-teal-900 dark:border-teal-900/50 dark:bg-teal-950/40 dark:text-teal-200">
            <RefreshCw className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
            <span>{progressStep}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Local Quick Download Section (Always Available) */}
        <div className="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/70 p-4 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-800/40">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
            <FileCode2 className="h-4 w-4 text-teal-600 dark:text-teal-400" />
            الملفات الجاهزة للنقل (41 ملف كود + قاعدة البيانات الشاملة)
          </div>
          <div className="mt-2.5 grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-3">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleDownloadLocalDb}
              className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200"
            >
              <span className="truncate">قاعدة البيانات JSON</span>
              <Download className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-teal-600" />
            </button>
            <a
              href={`/${CLOUD_SOURCE_TXT_FILENAME}`}
              download={CLOUD_SOURCE_TXT_FILENAME}
              className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200"
            >
              <span className="truncate">كل الأكواد (ملف نصي)</span>
              <Download className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-teal-600" />
            </a>
            <a
              href={`/${CLOUD_SOURCE_ZIP_FILENAME}`}
              download={CLOUD_SOURCE_ZIP_FILENAME}
              className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200"
            >
              <span className="truncate">المشروع كامل ZIP</span>
              <Download className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-teal-600" />
            </a>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Auth Section vs Connected Google Drive Transfer */}
        {needsAuth ? (
          <div className="mt-5 space-y-4 rounded-2xl border border-teal-200 bg-teal-50/40 p-6 text-center dark:border-teal-900/50 dark:bg-teal-950/20">
            <h3 className="font-display text-base font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
              سجّل الدخول بحساب Google لنقل الملفات إلى Drive الخاص بك
            </h3>
            <p className="text-xs leading-relaxed text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
              بمجرد تسجيل الدخول، اضغط على زر النقل وسيتم إنشاء مجلد{' '}
              <strong>«VetCarePro_بيطار_برو»</strong> في Google Drive الخاص بك ورفع قاعدة البيانات وكل الأكواد البرمجية إليه مباشرة.
            </p>

            <div className="flex justify-center pt-1">
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={isLoggingIn}
                onClick={handleLogin}
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800 shadow-xs transition-colors hover:bg-slate-50 disabled:opacity-60 dark:border-slate-600 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-700"
              >
                <svg
                  className="h-5 w-5"
                  viewBox="0 0 48 48"
                  xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
                >
                  <path
                    fill="#EA4335"
                    d="M24 9.5c3.54 0 6.71 1.22 9.21 3.6l6.85-6.85C35.9 2.38 30.47 0 24 0 14.62 0 6.51 5.38 2.56 13.22l7.98 6.19C12.43 13.72 17.74 9.5 24 9.5z"
                  />
                  <path
                    fill="#4285F4"
                    d="M46.98 24.55c0-1.57-.15-3.09-.38-4.55H24v9.02h12.94c-.58 2.96-2.26 5.48-4.78 7.18l7.73 6c4.51-4.18 7.09-10.36 7.09-17.65z"
                  />
                  <path
                    fill="#FBBC05"
                    d="M10.53 28.59c-.48-1.45-.76-2.99-.76-4.59s.27-3.14.76-4.59l-7.98-6.19C.92 16.46 0 20.12 0 24c0 3.88.92 7.54 2.56 10.78l7.97-6.19z"
                  />
                  <path
                    fill="#34A853"
                    d="M24 48c6.48 0 11.93-2.13 15.89-5.81l-7.73-6c-2.15 1.45-4.92 2.3-8.16 2.3-6.26 0-11.57-4.22-13.47-9.91l-7.98 6.19C6.51 42.62 14.62 48 24 48z"
                  />
                </svg>
                <span>
                  {isLoggingIn
                    ? 'جاري الاتصال بحساب Google...'
                    : 'Sign in with Google — الدخول لبدء النقل'}
                </span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="mt-5 space-y-5">
            {/* Connected User Bar */}
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-3.5 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-800/40">
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <div className="truncate text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                  متصل بحساب: {googleUser?.displayName || 'حساب Google'}
                </div>
                <div className="truncate font-mono-num text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                  {googleUser?.email || ''}
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={isBusy}
                  onClick={refreshCloudStatus}
                  title="تحديث الملفات"
                  className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white p-1.5 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-300"
                >
                  <RefreshCw
                    className={`h-3.5 w-3.5 ${isBusy ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`}
                  />
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleLogout}
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200"
                >
                  <LogOut className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                  خروج
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Primary 1-Click Transfer All CTA */}
            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-teal-200 bg-teal-50/50 p-5 dark:border-teal-900/60 dark:bg-teal-950/25">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
                <div>
                  <div className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
                    نقل قاعدة البيانات + كل الأكواد إلى Google Drive
                  </div>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
                    سيتم رفع{' '}
                    <strong className="font-mono-num">
                      {currentData.patients.length}
                    </strong>{' '}
                    مريض،{' '}
                    <strong className="font-mono-num">
                      {currentData.visits.length}
                    </strong>{' '}
                    فحص،{' '}
                    <strong className="font-mono-num">
                      {currentData.appointments.length}
                    </strong>{' '}
                    موعد، وجداول الأدوية والمتجر + جميع ملفات الكود.
                  </p>
                </div>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={isBusy}
                  onClick={() =>
                    setConfirmAction({
                      type: 'transfer_all',
                      isOverwrite: cloudFiles.length > 0,
                    })
                  }
                  className="inline-flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-teal-600 px-5 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-white transition-colors hover:bg-teal-700 disabled:opacity-50 sm:w-auto"
                >
                  <CloudUpload className="h-4 w-4" />
                  {cloudFiles.length > 0
                    ? 'تحديث ونقل الكل إلى Drive الآن'
                    : 'نقل كل شيء إلى Google Drive الآن'}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Cloud Files Status List */}
            {cloudFiles.length > 0 && (
              <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 dark:border-slate-800">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-slate-200 pb-3 dark:border-slate-800">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                    <FolderOpen className="h-4 w-4 text-teal-600" />
                    الملفات المنقولة في Google Drive ({cloudFiles.length})
                  </div>
                  {cloudFolder?.webViewLink && (
                    <a
                      href={cloudFolder.webViewLink}
                      target="_blank"
                      rel="noreferrer"
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-semibold text-teal-700 hover:underline dark:text-teal-400"
                    >
                      <ExternalLink className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                      فتح المجلد في Drive
                    </a>
                  )}
                </div>

                <div className="mt-2 divide-y divide-slate-100 dark:divide-slate-800">
                  {cloudFiles.map((file) => (
                    <div
                      key={file.id}
                      className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 py-2.5 text-xs"
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-emerald-600" />
                        <div>
                          <div className="font-mono-num font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                            {file.name}
                          </div>
                          {file.modifiedTime && (
                            <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">
                              آخر تحديث:{' '}
                              <span className="font-mono-num">
                                {new Date(file.modifiedTime).toLocaleString(
                                  'ar-SA'
                                )}
                              </span>
                            </div>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      {file.webViewLink && (
                        <a
                          href={file.webViewLink}
                          target="_blank"
                          rel="noreferrer"
                          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:border-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300"
                        >
                          <ExternalLink className="h-3 w-3" />
                          عرض
                        </a>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>

                <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2 border-t border-slate-200 pt-3 dark:border-slate-800">
                  {dbFile && (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      disabled={isBusy}
                      onClick={() =>
                        setConfirmAction({ type: 'restore_db', file: dbFile })
                      }
                      className="inline-flex flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:border-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200"
                    >
                      <CloudDownload className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                      استعادة قاعدة البيانات من Drive
                    </button>
                  )}
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    disabled={isBusy}
                    onClick={() =>
                      setConfirmAction({
                        type: 'delete_all',
                        files: cloudFiles,
                      })
                    }
                    className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-rose-200 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-rose-700 hover:bg-rose-50 dark:border-rose-900/50 dark:text-rose-400"
                  >
                    <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                    حذف الملفات من Drive
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Mandatory Confirmation Dialog */}
        {confirmAction && (
          <div className="fixed inset-0 z-60 flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-950/70 p-4">
            <div className="w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-2xl dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-400">
                <AlertTriangle className="h-5 w-5 shrink-0" />
                <h3 className="font-display text-base font-bold">
                  {confirmAction.type === 'transfer_all'
                    ? 'تأكيد النقل إلى Google Drive'
                    : confirmAction.type === 'restore_db'
                    ? 'تأكيد الاستيراد من Google Drive'
                    : 'تأكيد حذف ملفات Google Drive'}
                </h3>
              </div>

              <p className="mt-3 text-xs leading-relaxed text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
                {confirmAction.type === 'transfer_all'
                  ? confirmAction.isOverwrite
                    ? `سيتم تحديث واستبدال 3 ملفات في مجلد "VetCarePro_بيطار_برو" على Google Drive (${CLOUD_DB_FILENAME} و ${CLOUD_SOURCE_TXT_FILENAME} و ${CLOUD_SOURCE_ZIP_FILENAME}). هل تريد المتابعة؟`
                    : `سيتم إنشاء مجلد "VetCarePro_بيطار_برو" في Google Drive الخاص بك ورفع 3 ملفات إليه: قاعدة البيانات الكاملة (${CLOUD_DB_FILENAME})، ملف الأكواد النصي (${CLOUD_SOURCE_TXT_FILENAME})، وملف المشروع المضغوط (${CLOUD_SOURCE_ZIP_FILENAME}). هل تريد المتابعة؟`
                  : confirmAction.type === 'restore_db'
                  ? `سيتم استبدال البيانات الحالية على جهازك بالنسخة المحفوظة في "${confirmAction.file.name}" على Google Drive. هل تريد المتابعة؟`
                  : `هل أنت متأكد من حذف ${confirmAction.files.length} ملف(ات) نهائياً من Google Drive؟ لا يمكن التراجع عن هذا الإجراء.`}
              </p>

              <div className="mt-5 flex justify-end gap-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setConfirmAction(null)}
                  className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:border-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300"
                >
                  إلغاء
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={executeConfirmedAction}
                  className={`rounded-xl px-5 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-white ${
                    confirmAction.type === 'delete_all'
                      ? 'bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-700'
                      : 'bg-teal-600 hover:bg-teal-700'
                  }`}
                >
                  تأكيد وتنفيذ
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

--- FILE: src/components/InstallShareModal.tsx ---
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  CheckCircle2,
  Copy,
  Download,
  Monitor,
  Share2,
  Smartphone,
  WifiOff,
  X,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { useOnlineStatus, usePWAInstall } from '../hooks/usePWAInstall';

interface InstallShareModalProps {
  clinicName: string;
  onCopyText: (text: string) => void;
}

export const InstallShareButton: React.FC<InstallShareModalProps> = ({
  clinicName,
  onCopyText,
}) => {
  const { isInstallable, isInstalled, isIOS, install } = usePWAInstall();
  const [openModal, setOpenModal] = useState(false);
  const [installedJustNow, setInstalledJustNow] = useState(false);

  const shareUrl =
    typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.location.origin : '';

  const whatsappInvite = `أهلاً بكم في تطبيق "${clinicName} — بيطار برو"\nيمكنكم الآن فتح التطبيق، تسجيل حساب (مربي مواشي أو صاحب حيوان أليف)، متابعة التطعيمات، حجز المواعيد، والشراء من المتجر البيطري:\n${shareUrl}\n\nلتثبيت التطبيق على جوالك:\n• أندرويد (Chrome): افتح الرابط واضغط على القائمة (⋮) ثم "تثبيت التطبيق" أو "إضافة إلى الشاشة الرئيسية".\n• آيفون (Safari): افتح الرابط واضغط زر المشاركة (↑) ثم "إضافة إلى الصفحة الرئيسية".`;

  const handleDirectInstall = async () => {
    if (isInstallable) {
      const ok = await install();
      if (ok) {
        setInstalledJustNow(true);
        return;
      }
    }
    setOpenModal(true);
  };

  return (
    <>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => setOpenModal(true)}
        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap rounded-xl border border-teal-200 bg-teal-50/80 px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-teal-800 transition-colors hover:bg-teal-100 dark:border-teal-800 dark:bg-teal-950/50 dark:text-teal-300 dark:hover:bg-teal-900/50"
      >
        <Download className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
        {isInstalled ? 'مشاركة التطبيق' : 'تثبيت / مشاركة'}
      </button>

      {openModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-950/60 p-4 backdrop-blur-xs">
          <div className="max-h-[92vh] w-full max-w-xl overflow-y-auto rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-xl dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900">
            {/* Modal Header */}
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-200 pb-4 dark:border-slate-800">
              <div>
                <h2 className="font-display text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
                  تثبيت التطبيق ومشاركته مع عملائك
                </h2>
                <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                  يعمل التطبيق كتطبيق جوال وسطح مكتب مستقل (PWA) حتى عند ضعف الإنترنت
                </p>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setOpenModal(false)}
                className="rounded-lg p-1.5 text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800"
              >
                <X className="h-4 w-4" />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-5 space-y-5">
              {/* Direct Native Install Banner */}
              {isInstalled || installedJustNow ? (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50/70 p-4 text-xs text-emerald-900 dark:border-emerald-900/60 dark:bg-emerald-950/30 dark:text-emerald-200">
                  <CheckCircle2 className="h-5 w-5 shrink-0 text-emerald-600" />
                  <span>
                    التطبيق مثبت ويعمل بنجاح في وضع التطبيق المستقل على هذا الجهاز.
                  </span>
                </div>
              ) : isInstallable ? (
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 rounded-xl border border-teal-200 bg-teal-50/70 p-4 dark:border-teal-900/60 dark:bg-teal-950/30">
                  <div>
                    <div className="text-sm font-bold text-teal-950 dark:text-teal-100">
                      جاهز للتثبيت المباشر على جهازك
                    </div>
                    <div className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-teal-800 dark:text-teal-300">
                      اضغط لتثبيت أيقونة «بيطار برو» على الشاشة الرئيسية فوراً.
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={handleDirectInstall}
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-teal-600 px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-white hover:bg-teal-700"
                  >
                    <Download className="h-4 w-4" />
                    تثبيت الآن
                  </button>
                </div>
              ) : null}

              {/* Step-by-step Install Guides for All Devices */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
                <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/70 p-4 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-800/40">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                    <Smartphone className="h-4 w-4 text-emerald-600" />
                    تثبيت على أندرويد (Chrome)
                  </div>
                  <ol className="mt-2 space-y-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
                    <li>1. افتح رابط التطبيق في متصفح Google Chrome.</li>
                    <li>2. اضغط على أيقونة القائمة (⋮) في الأعلى.</li>
                    <li>
                      3. اختر <strong>«تثبيت التطبيق»</strong> أو{' '}
                      <strong>«الإضافة إلى الشاشة الرئيسية»</strong>.
                    </li>
                  </ol>
                </div>

                <div
                  className={`rounded-xl border p-4 ${
                    isIOS
                      ? 'border-teal-300 bg-teal-50/50 dark:border-teal-800 dark:bg-teal-950/30'
                      : 'border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/70 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-800/40'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                    <Smartphone className="h-4 w-4 text-teal-600" />
                    تثبيت على آيفون / آيباد (Safari)
                  </div>
                  <ol className="mt-2 space-y-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
                    <li>1. افتح الرابط في متصفح Safari.</li>
                    <li>2. اضغط على زر المشاركة (المربع بسهم لأعلى ↑).</li>
                    <li>
                      3. اختر <strong>«إضافة إلى الصفحة الرئيسية»</strong>.
                    </li>
                  </ol>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/70 p-4 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-800/40">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                  <Monitor className="h-4 w-4 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300" />
                  تثبيت على الكمبيوتر (Windows / Mac)
                </div>
                <p className="mt-1.5 text-xs leading-relaxed text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
                  في متصفح Chrome أو Edge، اضغط على أيقونة التثبيت (⊕) الموجودة في يمين/يسار شريط العنوان بالأعلى، أو من القائمة: حفظ ومشاركة ← تثبيت التطبيق.
                </p>
              </div>

              {/* Share with Clients Section */}
              <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 dark:border-slate-800">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                  <Share2 className="h-4 w-4 text-teal-600" />
                  مشاركة التطبيق مع عملائك (مربي المواشي وأصحاب الحيوانات)
                </div>
                <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
                  في منصة AI Studio، تأكد من نشر/مشاركة التطبيق من زر <strong>Share</strong> في أعلى المنصة ثم أرسل الرابط لعملائك لتسجيل حساباتهم وتثبيت التطبيق على هواتفهم:
                </p>

                <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    readOnly
                    value={shareUrl}
                    dir="ltr"
                    className="flex-1 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 px-3 py-2 font-mono-num text-xs text-slate-700 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200"
                  />
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => onCopyText(shareUrl)}
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:border-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 dark:hover:bg-slate-800"
                  >
                    <Copy className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                    نسخ الرابط
                  </button>
                </div>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => onCopyText(whatsappInvite)}
                  className="mt-3 flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-white transition-colors hover:bg-emerald-700"
                >
                  <Copy className="h-4 w-4" />
                  نسخ رسالة دعوة واتساب جاهزة للعملاء (تشمل طريقة التثبيت)
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
};

export const OfflineIndicator: React.FC = () => {
  const isOnline = useOnlineStatus();
  if (isOnline) return null;

  return (
    <div
      role="status"
      className="no-print fixed bottom-5 right-5 z-50 flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-amber-300 bg-amber-500 px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-950 shadow-lg"
    >
      <WifiOff className="h-4 w-4" />
      <span>وضع عدم الاتصال — يتم استخدام البيانات المحفوظة محلياً</span>
    </div>
  );
};

--- FILE: src/components/OverviewTab.tsx ---
import React from 'react';
import {
  AlertTriangle,
  Calculator,
  Calendar,
  CheckCircle2,
  Clock,
  Copy,
  FilePlus2,
  Stethoscope,
  Syringe,
  Users,
} from 'lucide-react';
import {
  APPOINTMENT_TYPE_LABELS,
  SPECIES_LABELS,
  VISIT_STATUS_LABELS,
} from '../data/vetData';
import {
  Appointment,
  Patient,
  SoapVisit,
  VaccinationRecord,
  VisitStatus,
} from '../types/vet';

interface OverviewTabProps {
  todayStr: string;
  patients: Patient[];
  appointments: Appointment[];
  visits: SoapVisit[];
  vaccinations: VaccinationRecord[];
  onUpdateVisitStatus: (appointmentId: string, status: VisitStatus) => void;
  onStartExam: (patientId: string) => void;
  onOpenDosageForPatient: (patientId: string) => void;
  onCopyReminder: (text: string) => void;
  onNavigatePatients: () => void;
  onNavigateSchedule: () => void;
  onMarkVaccineDone: (vacId: string) => void;
}

const STATUS_STYLE: Record<
  VisitStatus,
  { text: string; colorClass: string; Icon: React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }> }
> = {
  waiting: {
    text: 'في الانتظار',
    colorClass: 'text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-400',
    Icon: Clock,
  },
  in_exam: {
    text: 'قيد الفحص',
    colorClass: 'text-teal-700 dark:text-teal-400',
    Icon: Stethoscope,
  },
  completed: {
    text: 'مكتمل',
    colorClass: 'text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400',
    Icon: CheckCircle2,
  },
  urgent: {
    text: 'حالة طارئة',
    colorClass: 'text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-400',
    Icon: AlertTriangle,
  },
};

export const OverviewTab: React.FC<OverviewTabProps> = ({
  todayStr,
  patients,
  appointments,
  visits,
  vaccinations,
  onUpdateVisitStatus,
  onStartExam,
  onOpenDosageForPatient,
  onCopyReminder,
  onNavigatePatients,
  onNavigateSchedule,
  onMarkVaccineDone,
}) => {
  const todayAppointments = appointments
    .filter((a) => a.date === todayStr && a.status !== 'cancelled')
    .sort((a, b) => a.time.localeCompare(b.time));

  const urgentCount = todayAppointments.filter(
    (a) => a.visitStatus === 'urgent'
  ).length;

  const dueSoonVaccines = vaccinations
    .filter((v) => !v.completed)
    .sort((a, b) => a.nextDueDate.localeCompare(b.nextDueDate));

  const getPatient = (id: string) => patients.find((p) => p.id === id);

  const daysUntil = (targetDate: string) => {
    const start = new Date(todayStr + 'T00:00:00');
    const end = new Date(targetDate + 'T00:00:00');
    return Math.round((end.getTime() - start.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
  };

  const buildVaccineReminder = (v: VaccinationRecord, p: Patient) => {
    return `السلام عليكم أ/ ${p.ownerName}، نذكركم بموعد تحصين (${v.vaccineName}) لـ "${p.name}" بتاريخ ${v.nextDueDate}. يسعدنا تواصلكم لتأكيد الموعد.`;
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-8">
      {/* KPI Metrics Row */}
      <section aria-label="ملخص العيادة اليوم">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
          <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
              <span>مواعيد اليوم</span>
              <Calendar className="h-4 w-4 text-teal-600 dark:text-teal-400" />
            </div>
            <div className="mt-3 flex items-baseline justify-between">
              <span className="font-mono-num text-3xl font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
                {todayAppointments.length}
              </span>
              <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                {urgentCount > 0 ? `${urgentCount} حالة طارئة` : 'جدول مستقر'}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
              <span>المرضى المسجلون</span>
              <Users className="h-4 w-4 text-teal-600 dark:text-teal-400" />
            </div>
            <div className="mt-3 flex items-baseline justify-between">
              <span className="font-mono-num text-3xl font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
                {patients.length}
              </span>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onNavigatePatients}
                className="text-xs font-medium text-teal-700 hover:underline dark:text-teal-400"
              >
                عرض السجل
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
              <span>تحصينات مستحقة</span>
              <Syringe className="h-4 w-4 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400" />
            </div>
            <div className="mt-3 flex items-baseline justify-between">
              <span className="font-mono-num text-3xl font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
                {dueSoonVaccines.length}
              </span>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onNavigateSchedule}
                className="text-xs font-medium text-teal-700 hover:underline dark:text-teal-400"
              >
                إدارة التحصينات
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
              <span>سجلات الفحص SOAP</span>
              <FilePlus2 className="h-4 w-4 text-teal-600 dark:text-teal-400" />
            </div>
            <div className="mt-3 flex items-baseline justify-between">
              <span className="font-mono-num text-3xl font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
                {visits.length}
              </span>
              <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                موثقة سريرياً
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Main Split: Today's Queue + Due Vaccines */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 xl:grid-cols-3">
        {/* Today's Clinic & Field Queue */}
        <section className="xl:col-span-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 border-b border-slate-200 px-6 py-4 dark:border-slate-800">
            <div>
              <h2 className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
                قائمة الفحص والزيارات اليومية
              </h2>
              <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                تحديث حالة الدور وبدء الفحص السريري أو حساب الجرعة مباشرة
              </p>
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onNavigateSchedule}
              className="whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-50 dark:border-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 dark:hover:bg-slate-800"
            >
              إضافة موعد جديد
            </button>
          </div>

          {todayAppointments.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="px-6 py-12 text-center">
              <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                لا توجد مواعيد مسجلة لتاريخ اليوم ({todayStr})
              </p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                يمكنك إضافة موعد جديد من قسم المواعيد والتحصينات أو بدء فحص مباشر من ملفات المرضى.
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full text-right text-sm">
                <thead>
                  <tr className="border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/70 text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-800/40 dark:text-slate-400">
                    <th className="px-5 py-3 font-medium">الوقت</th>
                    <th className="px-5 py-3 font-medium">الحالة / الحيوان</th>
                    <th className="px-5 py-3 font-medium">سبب الزيارة</th>
                    <th className="px-5 py-3 font-medium">حالة الدور</th>
                    <th className="px-5 py-3 font-medium">إجراء سريري</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-200 dark:divide-slate-800">
                  {todayAppointments.map((apt) => {
                    const patient = getPatient(apt.patientId);
                    if (!patient) return null;
                    const vStatus: VisitStatus = apt.visitStatus || 'waiting';
                    const statusMeta = STATUS_STYLE[vStatus];
                    const StatusIcon = statusMeta.Icon;

                    return (
                      <tr
                        key={apt.id}
                        className="transition-colors hover:bg-slate-50/80 dark:hover:bg-slate-800/40"
                      >
                        <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-3.5 font-mono-num text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                          {apt.time}
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-5 py-3.5">
                          <div className="font-medium text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                            {patient.name}
                          </div>
                          <div className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                            {SPECIES_LABELS[patient.species]} ·{' '}
                            <span className="font-mono-num">{patient.weightKg} كغ</span> ·{' '}
                            {patient.ownerName}
                          </div>
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-5 py-3.5">
                          <div className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">
                            {APPOINTMENT_TYPE_LABELS[apt.type]}
                          </div>
                          <div className="mt-0.5 max-w-xs truncate text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                            {apt.reason}
                          </div>
                        </td>
                        <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-3.5">
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                            <StatusIcon className={`h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 ${statusMeta.colorClass}`} />
                            <select
                              aria-label={`حالة الدور لـ ${patient.name}`}
                              value={vStatus}
                              onChange={(e) =>
                                onUpdateVisitStatus(apt.id, e.target.value as VisitStatus)
                              }
                              className={`cursor-pointer bg-transparent text-xs font-medium focus:outline-none ${statusMeta.colorClass}`}
                            >
                              {Object.entries(VISIT_STATUS_LABELS).map(([k, label]) => (
                                <option
                                  key={k}
                                  value={k}
                                  className="bg-white text-slate-900 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100"
                                >
                                  {label}
                                </option>
                              ))}
                            </select>
                          </div>
                        </td>
                        <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-3.5">
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              onClick={() => onStartExam(patient.id)}
                              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg bg-teal-600 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-white transition-colors hover:bg-teal-700"
                            >
                              <Stethoscope className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                              فحص SOAP
                            </button>
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              onClick={() => onOpenDosageForPatient(patient.id)}
                              title="حساب الجرعة الدوائية لهذا الحيوان"
                              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 dark:hover:bg-slate-800"
                            >
                              <Calculator className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                              جرعة
                            </button>
                          </div>
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    );
                  })}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          )}
        </section>

        {/* Due Vaccinations & Owner Reminders */}
        <section className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900">
          <div className="border-b border-slate-200 px-6 py-4 dark:border-slate-800">
            <h2 className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
              تنبيهات اللقاحات والمتابعة
            </h2>
            <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
              نسخ رسالة التذكير للمربي أو تأكيد إتمام التحصين
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="divide-y divide-slate-200 dark:divide-slate-800">
            {dueSoonVaccines.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="px-6 py-10 text-center text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                جميع التحصينات المسجلة محدثة ومكتملة.
              </div>
            ) : (
              dueSoonVaccines.map((vac) => {
                const patient = getPatient(vac.patientId);
                if (!patient) return null;
                const diff = daysUntil(vac.nextDueDate);
                const urgencyText =
                  diff < 0
                    ? `متأخر بـ ${Math.abs(diff)} يوم`
                    : diff === 0
                    ? 'مستحق اليوم'
                    : `خلال ${diff} أيام`;
                const urgencyColor =
                  diff <= 0
                    ? 'text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-400'
                    : diff <= 7
                    ? 'text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-400'
                    : 'text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400';

                return (
                  <div key={vac.id} className="px-6 py-4">
                    <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                      <div>
                        <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                          {patient.name} · {SPECIES_LABELS[patient.species]}
                        </div>
                        <div className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
                          {vac.vaccineName}
                        </div>
                        <div className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                          <span>المربي: {patient.ownerName}</span>
                          <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                          <span className="font-mono-num">{vac.nextDueDate}</span>
                          <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                          <span className={`font-medium ${urgencyColor}`}>{urgencyText}</span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-2">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => onCopyReminder(buildVaccineReminder(vac, patient))}
                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-50 dark:border-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 dark:hover:bg-slate-800"
                      >
                        <Copy className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        نسخ رسالة التذكير
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => onMarkVaccineDone(vac.id)}
                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-teal-700 transition-colors hover:bg-teal-50 dark:text-teal-400 dark:hover:bg-teal-950/50"
                      >
                        <CheckCircle2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        تم التحصين
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })
            )}
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

--- FILE: src/components/OwnerPortalView.tsx ---
import React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Calendar,
  CalendarPlus,
  CheckCircle2,
  MapPin,
  PawPrint,
  Plus,
  ShieldAlert,
  ShoppingBag,
  Sprout,
  Syringe,
  X,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { APPOINTMENT_TYPE_LABELS, SPECIES_LABELS } from '../data/vetData';
import {
  Appointment,
  AppointmentType,
  CartItem,
  Patient,
  Sex,
  SoapVisit,
  Species,
  StoreOrder,
  UserAccount,
  VaccinationRecord,
} from '../types/vet';
import { StoreTab } from './StoreTab';

interface OwnerPortalViewProps {
  todayStr: string;
  account: UserAccount;
  patients: Patient[];
  visits: SoapVisit[];
  vaccinations: VaccinationRecord[];
  appointments: Appointment[];
  cart: CartItem[];
  orders: StoreOrder[];
  onAddPatient: (patient: Omit<Patient, 'id' | 'createdAt'>) => Patient;
  onAddAppointment: (apt: Omit<Appointment, 'id'>) => void;
  onAddToCart: (productId: string, variant: string) => void;
  onUpdateCartQty: (productId: string, variant: string, delta: number) => void;
  onRemoveFromCart: (productId: string, variant: string) => void;
  onPlaceOrder: (
    order: Omit<StoreOrder, 'id' | 'orderNumber' | 'date' | 'status'>
  ) => StoreOrder;
}

export const OwnerPortalView: React.FC<OwnerPortalViewProps> = ({
  todayStr,
  account,
  patients,
  visits,
  vaccinations,
  appointments,
  cart,
  orders,
  onAddPatient,
  onAddAppointment,
  onAddToCart,
  onUpdateCartQty,
  onRemoveFromCart,
  onPlaceOrder,
}) => {
  const isBreeder = account.role === 'livestock_breeder';

  const [portalTab, setPortalTab] = useState<
    'animals' | 'health' | 'booking' | 'store'
  >('animals');
  const [showAddAnimal, setShowAddAnimal] = useState(false);

  // Add Animal Form
  const [aName, setAName] = useState('');
  const [aSpecies, setASpecies] = useState<Species>(
    isBreeder ? Species.SheepGoat : Species.Cat
  );
  const [aBreed, setABreed] = useState('');
  const [aWeight, setAWeight] = useState(isBreeder ? '50' : '4');
  const [aAge, setAAge] = useState('');

  // Book Visit Form
  const [bPatientId, setBPatientId] = useState('');
  const [bDate, setBDate] = useState(todayStr);
  const [bTime, setBTime] = useState('10:00');
  const [bType, setBType] = useState<AppointmentType>(
    isBreeder ? 'farm_visit' : 'exam'
  );
  const [bReason, setBReason] = useState('');
